   189→  const hasFilters = filtersHook.filterByToolType || filtersHook.mediaTypeFilter !== 'all' || !!filtersHook.searchTerm.trim() || filtersHook.showStarredOnly || !filtersHook.toolTypeFilterEnabled;
   190→  const paginationHook = useMediaGalleryPagination({
   191→    filteredImages: filtersHook.filteredImages,
   192→    itemsPerPage: actualItemsPerPage,

   201→  const withPaginationReset = useCallback(<T,>(fn: () => T): T => {

   204→    if (paginationHook.isServerPagination) {

   209→  const handlePageBoundsExceeded = useCallback((newLastPage: number) => {

   214→  const actionsHook = useMediaGalleryActions({

   218→    onAddToLastShotWithoutPosition,

   226→    setDownloadingImageId: stateHook.setDownloadingImageId,

   228→    setShowTickForSecondaryImageId: stateHook.setShowTickForSecondaryImageId,
   229→    mainTickTimeoutRef: stateHook.mainTickTimeoutRef,
   230→    secondaryTickTimeoutRef: stateHook.secondaryTickTimeoutRef,
   231→    onBackfillRequest,
   232→    serverPage,
   233→    itemsPerPage: actualItemsPerPage,
   234→    isServerPagination: paginationHook.isServerPagination,
   235→    setIsBackfillLoading: stateHook.setIsBackfillLoading,

   237→    setIsDownloadingStarred: stateHook.setIsDownloadingStarred,
   238→    setSelectedShotIdLocal: stateHook.setSelectedShotIdLocal,
   239→    totalCount,
   240→    optimisticDeletedCount: stateHook.state.optimisticDeletedIds.size,
   241→    onPageBoundsExceeded: handlePageBoundsExceeded,

   243→  const shotAdditionSelection = useShotAdditionSelectionOptional();
   244→  useEffect(() => {

   249→    actionsHook.handleShotChange(selectedShotId);
   250→    shotAdditionSelection.clearSelectedShotForAddition();
   251→  }, [actionsHook, shotAdditionSelection]);
   252→  const lightboxImageId = getGenerationId(stateHook.state.activeLightboxMedia);
   253→  const {

   293→    prevFilteredImagesRef.current = currentSignature;
   294→    if (stateHook.state.pendingLightboxTarget && filtersHook.filteredImages.length > 0) {
   295→      const targetIndex = stateHook.state.pendingLightboxTarget === 'first' ? 0 : filtersHook.filteredImages.length - 1;

   481→        />

    52→  showTickForSecondaryImageId: MediaGalleryState['showTickForSecondaryImageId'];
    53→  addingToShotImageId: MediaGalleryState['addingToShotImageId'];
    54→  addingToShotWithoutPositionImageId: MediaGalleryState['addingToShotWithoutPositionImageId'];
    55→  downloadingImageId: MediaGalleryState['downloadingImageId'];
    56→  isDownloadingStarred: MediaGalleryState['isDownloadingStarred'];
    57→  mobileActiveImageId: MediaGalleryState['mobileActiveImageId'];
    58→  mobilePopoverOpenImageId: MediaGalleryState['mobilePopoverOpenImageId'];
    59→  isBackfillLoading: MediaGalleryState['isBackfillLoading'];
    60→  backfillSkeletonCount: MediaGalleryState['backfillSkeletonCount'];
    61→};
    62→
    63→type SetFieldAction = {
    64→  [Field in keyof SettableMediaGalleryFields]: {
    65→    type: 'SET_FIELD';
    66→    field: Field;
    67→    payload: SettableMediaGalleryFields[Field];
    68→  };
    69→}[keyof SettableMediaGalleryFields];
    70→
    71→// Action types for the reducer
    72→type MediaGalleryStateAction =
    73→  | SetFieldAction
    74→  | { type: 'MARK_OPTIMISTIC_UNPOSITIONED'; payload: { mediaId: string; shotId: string } }
    75→  | { type: 'MARK_OPTIMISTIC_POSITIONED'; payload: { mediaId: string; shotId: string } }
    76→  | { type: 'MARK_OPTIMISTIC_DELETED'; payload: string }
    77→  | { type: 'MARK_OPTIMISTIC_DELETED_WITH_BACKFILL'; payload: string } // Combined action for atomic update
    78→  | { type: 'REMOVE_OPTIMISTIC_DELETED'; payload: string }
    79→  | { type: 'RECONCILE_OPTIMISTIC_STATE'; payload: Set<string> }
    80→  | { type: 'RESET_UI_STATE' };
    81→
    82→// Initial state factory
    83→const createInitialState = (
    84→  currentShotId?: string,
    85→  lastShotId?: string,
    86→  simplifiedShotOptions: { id: string; name: string }[] = []
    87→): MediaGalleryState => ({
    88→  // Lightbox state
    89→  activeLightboxMedia: null,
    90→  autoEnterEditMode: false,
    91→  selectedImageForDetails: null,
    92→  showTaskDetailsModal: false,
    93→  pendingLightboxTarget: null,
    94→  
    95→  // Optimistic state
    96→  optimisticUnpositionedIds: new Set(),
    97→  optimisticPositionedIds: new Set(),
    98→  optimisticDeletedIds: new Set(),
    99→  
   100→  // Shot selection state
   101→  selectedShotIdLocal: currentShotId || lastShotId || (simplifiedShotOptions.length > 0 ? simplifiedShotOptions[0].id : ""),
   102→  
   103→  // UI state
   104→  showTickForImageId: null,
   105→  showTickForSecondaryImageId: null,
   106→  addingToShotImageId: null,
   107→  addingToShotWithoutPositionImageId: null,
   108→  downloadingImageId: null,
   109→  isDownloadingStarred: false,
   110→  
   111→  // Mobile state
   112→  mobileActiveImageId: null,
   113→  mobilePopoverOpenImageId: null,
   114→  
   115→  // Backfill state
   116→  isBackfillLoading: false,
   117→  backfillSkeletonCount: 0,
   118→});
   119→
   120→// Optimized reducer with batched updates
   121→const mediaGalleryStateReducer = (
   122→  state: MediaGalleryState,
   123→  action: MediaGalleryStateAction
   124→): MediaGalleryState => {
   125→  switch (action.type) {
   126→    case 'SET_FIELD':
   127→      return {
   128→        ...state,
   129→        [action.field]: action.payload,
   130→      };
   131→      
   132→    case 'MARK_OPTIMISTIC_UNPOSITIONED': {
   133→      // Store composite key: mediaId:shotId
   134→      const { mediaId, shotId } = action.payload;
   135→      const key = `${mediaId}:${shotId}`;
   136→      const newUnpositioned = new Set(state.optimisticUnpositionedIds);
   137→      const newPositioned = new Set(state.optimisticPositionedIds);
   138→      newUnpositioned.add(key);
   139→      newPositioned.delete(key);
   140→      return {
   141→        ...state,
   142→        optimisticUnpositionedIds: newUnpositioned,
   143→        optimisticPositionedIds: newPositioned,
   144→      };
   145→    }
   146→    
   147→    case 'MARK_OPTIMISTIC_POSITIONED': {
   148→      // Store composite key: mediaId:shotId
   149→      const { mediaId, shotId } = action.payload;
   150→      const key = `${mediaId}:${shotId}`;
   151→      const newPositioned = new Set(state.optimisticPositionedIds);
   152→      const newUnpositioned = new Set(state.optimisticUnpositionedIds);
   153→      newPositioned.add(key);
   154→      newUnpositioned.delete(key);
   155→      return {
   156→        ...state,
   157→        optimisticPositionedIds: newPositioned,
   158→        optimisticUnpositionedIds: newUnpositioned,
   159→      };
   160→    }
   161→    
   162→    case 'MARK_OPTIMISTIC_DELETED': {
   163→      const newDeleted = new Set(state.optimisticDeletedIds);
   164→      newDeleted.add(action.payload);
   165→      return { ...state, optimisticDeletedIds: newDeleted };
   166→    }
   167→
   168→    case 'MARK_OPTIMISTIC_DELETED_WITH_BACKFILL': {
   169→      // Combined action: mark deleted AND enable backfill loading in ONE state update
   170→      // This ensures skeleton appears in the same render where item disappears
   171→      const newDeleted = new Set(state.optimisticDeletedIds);
   172→      newDeleted.add(action.payload);
   173→      return { ...state, optimisticDeletedIds: newDeleted, isBackfillLoading: true };
   174→    }
   175→
   176→    case 'REMOVE_OPTIMISTIC_DELETED': {
   177→      const newDeleted = new Set(state.optimisticDeletedIds);
   178→      newDeleted.delete(action.payload);
   179→      return { ...state, optimisticDeletedIds: newDeleted };
   180→    }
   181→    
   182→    case 'RECONCILE_OPTIMISTIC_STATE': {
   183→      const currentImageIds = action.payload;
   184→
   185→      // Clean up optimistic sets - remove entries for images no longer in the list
   186→      // Composite keys are in format mediaId:shotId
   187→      const newUnpositioned = new Set<string>();
   188→      for (const key of state.optimisticUnpositionedIds) {
   189→        const mediaId = key.split(':')[0];
   190→        if (currentImageIds.has(mediaId)) {
   191→          newUnpositioned.add(key);
   192→        }
   193→      }
   194→
   195→      const newPositioned = new Set<string>();
   196→      for (const key of state.optimisticPositionedIds) {
   197→        const mediaId = key.split(':')[0];
   198→        if (currentImageIds.has(mediaId)) {
   199→          newPositioned.add(key);
   200→        }
   201→      }
   202→
   203→      const newDeleted = new Set<string>();
   204→      for (const id of state.optimisticDeletedIds) {
   205→        if (currentImageIds.has(id)) {
   206→          newDeleted.add(id);
   207→        }
   208→      }
   209→      
   210→      return {
   211→        ...state,
   212→        optimisticUnpositionedIds: newUnpositioned,
   213→        optimisticPositionedIds: newPositioned,
   214→        optimisticDeletedIds: newDeleted,
   215→      };
   216→    }
   217→    
   218→    case 'RESET_UI_STATE':
   219→      return {
   220→        ...state,
   221→        showTickForImageId: null,
   222→        showTickForSecondaryImageId: null,
   223→        addingToShotImageId: null,
   224→        addingToShotWithoutPositionImageId: null,
   225→        downloadingImageId: null,
   226→        isDownloadingStarred: false,
   227→        mobileActiveImageId: null,
   228→        mobilePopoverOpenImageId: null,
   229→      };
   230→      
   231→    default:
   232→      return state;
   233→  }
   234→};
   235→
   236→const createFieldSetter = <Field extends keyof SettableMediaGalleryFields>(
   237→  dispatch: Dispatch<MediaGalleryStateAction>,
   238→  field: Field
   239→) => (value: SettableMediaGalleryFields[Field]) => {
   240→  dispatch({
   241→    type: 'SET_FIELD',
   242→    field,
   243→    payload: value,
   244→  });
   245→};
   246→
   247→interface UseMediaGalleryStateOptimizedProps {
   248→  images: GeneratedImageWithMetadata[];
   249→  currentShotId?: string;
   250→  lastShotId?: string;
   251→  simplifiedShotOptions: { id: string; name: string }[];
   252→  isServerPagination?: boolean;
   253→  serverPage?: number;
   254→}
   255→
   256→interface UseMediaGalleryStateOptimizedReturn {
   257→  // State
   258→  state: MediaGalleryState;
   259→  
   260→  // Actions
   261→  setActiveLightboxMedia: (media: GeneratedImageWithMetadata | null) => void;
   262→  setAutoEnterEditMode: (value: boolean) => void;
   263→  setSelectedImageForDetails: (image: GeneratedImageWithMetadata | null) => void;
   264→  setShowTaskDetailsModal: (show: boolean) => void;
   265→  setPendingLightboxTarget: (target: 'first' | 'last' | null) => void;
   266→  markOptimisticUnpositioned: (imageId: string, shotId: string) => void;
   267→  markOptimisticPositioned: (imageId: string, shotId: string) => void;
   268→  markOptimisticDeleted: (imageId: string) => void;
   269→  markOptimisticDeletedWithBackfill: (imageId: string) => void;
   270→  removeOptimisticDeleted: (imageId: string) => void;
   271→  setSelectedShotIdLocal: (id: string) => void;
   272→  setShowTickForImageId: (id: string | null) => void;
   273→  setShowTickForSecondaryImageId: (id: string | null) => void;
   274→  setAddingToShotImageId: (id: string | null) => void;
   275→  setAddingToShotWithoutPositionImageId: (id: string | null) => void;
   276→  setDownloadingImageId: (id: string | null) => void;
   277→  setIsDownloadingStarred: (downloading: boolean) => void;
   278→  setMobileActiveImageId: (id: string | null) => void;
   279→  setMobilePopoverOpenImageId: (id: string | null) => void;
   280→  setIsBackfillLoading: (loading: boolean) => void;
   281→  setBackfillSkeletonCount: (count: number) => void;
   282→  resetUIState: () => void;
   283→  
   284→  // Refs (unchanged)
   285→  mainTickTimeoutRef: React.MutableRefObject<NodeJS.Timeout | null>;
   286→  secondaryTickTimeoutRef: React.MutableRefObject<NodeJS.Timeout | null>;
   287→  galleryTopRef: React.MutableRefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>;
   288→  safetyTimeoutRef: React.MutableRefObject<NodeJS.Timeout | null>;
   289→}
   290→
   291→function clearTimer(timeoutRef: React.MutableRefObject<NodeJS.Timeout | null>): void {
   292→  const timeoutId = timeoutRef.current;
   293→  if (!timeoutId) {
   294→    return;
   295→  }
   296→
   297→  clearTimeout(timeoutId);
   298→  timeoutRef.current = null;
   299→}
   300→
   301→export const useMediaGalleryStateOptimized = ({
   302→  images,
   303→  currentShotId,
   304→  lastShotId,
   305→  simplifiedShotOptions,
   306→}: UseMediaGalleryStateOptimizedProps): UseMediaGalleryStateOptimizedReturn => {
   307→  
   308→  // Initialize state with useReducer instead of multiple useState calls
   309→  const [state, dispatch] = useReducer(
   310→    mediaGalleryStateReducer,
   311→    createInitialState(currentShotId, lastShotId, simplifiedShotOptions)
   312→  );
   313→  
   314→  // Memoized action creators to prevent unnecessary re-renders
   315→  const actions = useMemo(() => ({
   316→    setActiveLightboxMedia: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'activeLightboxMedia'),
   317→    setAutoEnterEditMode: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'autoEnterEditMode'),
   318→    setSelectedImageForDetails: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'selectedImageForDetails'),
   319→    setShowTaskDetailsModal: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'showTaskDetailsModal'),
   320→    setPendingLightboxTarget: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'pendingLightboxTarget'),
   321→    markOptimisticUnpositioned: (imageId: string, shotId: string) => 
   322→      dispatch({ type: 'MARK_OPTIMISTIC_UNPOSITIONED', payload: { mediaId: imageId, shotId } }),
   323→    markOptimisticPositioned: (imageId: string, shotId: string) => 
   324→      dispatch({ type: 'MARK_OPTIMISTIC_POSITIONED', payload: { mediaId: imageId, shotId } }),
   325→    markOptimisticDeleted: (imageId: string) =>
   326→      dispatch({ type: 'MARK_OPTIMISTIC_DELETED', payload: imageId }),
   327→    markOptimisticDeletedWithBackfill: (imageId: string) =>
   328→      dispatch({ type: 'MARK_OPTIMISTIC_DELETED_WITH_BACKFILL', payload: imageId }),
   329→    removeOptimisticDeleted: (imageId: string) => 
   330→      dispatch({ type: 'REMOVE_OPTIMISTIC_DELETED', payload: imageId }),
   331→    setSelectedShotIdLocal: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'selectedShotIdLocal'),
   332→    setShowTickForImageId: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'showTickForImageId'),
   333→    setShowTickForSecondaryImageId: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'showTickForSecondaryImageId'),
   334→    setAddingToShotImageId: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'addingToShotImageId'),
   335→    setAddingToShotWithoutPositionImageId: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'addingToShotWithoutPositionImageId'),
   336→    setDownloadingImageId: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'downloadingImageId'),
   337→    setIsDownloadingStarred: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'isDownloadingStarred'),
   338→    setMobileActiveImageId: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'mobileActiveImageId'),
   339→    setMobilePopoverOpenImageId: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'mobilePopoverOpenImageId'),
   340→    setIsBackfillLoading: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'isBackfillLoading'),
   341→    setBackfillSkeletonCount: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'backfillSkeletonCount'),
   342→    resetUIState: () =>
   343→      dispatch({ type: 'RESET_UI_STATE' }),
   344→  }), []);
   345→  
   346→  // Refs (unchanged from original)
   347→  const mainTickTimeoutRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);
   348→  const secondaryTickTimeoutRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);
   349→  const galleryTopRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
   350→  const safetyTimeoutRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);
   351→
   352→  // Track previous lastShotId to detect when user navigates to a different shot
   353→  const prevLastShotIdRef = useRef<string | undefined>(lastShotId);
   354→  
   355→  // Sync selectedShotIdLocal when:
   356→  // 1. lastShotId changes (user navigated to a different shot)
   357→  // 2. Current selection is invalid (empty or shot no longer exists)
   358→  useEffect(() => {
   359→    const isCurrentSelectionValid = state.selectedShotIdLocal && simplifiedShotOptions.find(shot => shot.id === state.selectedShotIdLocal);
   360→    const lastShotIdChanged = lastShotId && lastShotId !== prevLastShotIdRef.current;
   361→    
   362→    // Update ref for next comparison
   363→    prevLastShotIdRef.current = lastShotId;
   364→    
   365→    // Sync when lastShotId changes (user clicked into a shot)
   366→    if (lastShotIdChanged && lastShotId !== state.selectedShotIdLocal) {
   367→      actions.setSelectedShotIdLocal(lastShotId);
   368→      return;
   369→    }
   370→    
   371→    // Fix invalid selections (empty or shot no longer exists)
   372→    if (!isCurrentSelectionValid) {
   373→      const newSelection = lastShotId || (simplifiedShotOptions.length > 0 ? simplifiedShotOptions[0].id : "");
   374→      if (newSelection && newSelection !== state.selectedShotIdLocal) {
   375→        actions.setSelectedShotIdLocal(newSelection);
   376→      }
   377→    }
   378→  }, [lastShotId, simplifiedShotOptions, state.selectedShotIdLocal, actions]);
   379→
   380→  // Memoize image IDs to prevent unnecessary effect triggers
   381→  const currentImageIds = useMemo(() => 
   382→    new Set(images.map(img => img.id)), 
   383→    [images]
   384→  );
   385→
   386→  // Reconcile optimistic state when images update
   387→  useEffect(() => {
   388→    // Sync activeLightboxMedia with updated images list to ensure fresh data (like name changes)
   389→    if (state.activeLightboxMedia) {
   390→      const activeLightbox = state.activeLightboxMedia;
   391→      const updatedImage = images.find(img => img.id === activeLightbox.id);
   392→      // Only update if the object reference changed (meaning data changed or refetched)
   393→      // and deep equality check on key properties to avoid unnecessary render cycles
   394→      if (updatedImage && updatedImage !== state.activeLightboxMedia) {
   395→        // Check if relevant fields actually changed to prevent loop.
   396→        const nameChanged = updatedImage.name !== state.activeLightboxMedia.name;
   397→        const starredChanged = updatedImage.starred !== state.activeLightboxMedia.starred;
   398→        const urlChanged = updatedImage.url !== state.activeLightboxMedia.url;
   399→
   400→        if (nameChanged || starredChanged || urlChanged) {
   401→          const autoEnterFlag = state.activeLightboxMedia.metadata?.__autoEnterEditMode as boolean | undefined;
   402→          actions.setActiveLightboxMedia({
   403→            ...updatedImage,
   404→            metadata: autoEnterFlag === undefined
   405→              ? updatedImage.metadata
   406→              : { ...updatedImage.metadata, __autoEnterEditMode: autoEnterFlag },
   407→          });
   408→        }
   409→      }
   410→    }
   411→
   412→    // Clean up optimistic sets using the consolidated action
   413→    dispatch({ type: 'RECONCILE_OPTIMISTIC_STATE', payload: currentImageIds });
   414→  }, [currentImageIds, images, actions, state.activeLightboxMedia]);
   415→
   416→  // Cleanup timeouts on unmount
   417→  useEffect(() => {
   418→    return () => {
   419→      clearTimer(mainTickTimeoutRef);
   420→      clearTimer(secondaryTickTimeoutRef);
   421→      clearTimer(safetyTimeoutRef);
   422→    };
   423→  }, []);
   424→
   425→  return {
   426→    state,
   427→    ...actions,
   428→    
   429→    // Refs
   430→    mainTickTimeoutRef,
   431→    secondaryTickTimeoutRef,
   432→    galleryTopRef,
   433→    safetyTimeoutRef,
   434→  };
   435→};
   436→

1→import { useReducer, useCallback, useMemo, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
     2→import type { GeneratedImageWithMetadata } from '../types';
     3→import { GalleryFilterState, DEFAULT_GALLERY_FILTERS } from '../types';
     4→import { hasVideoExtension } from '@/shared/lib/typeGuards';
     5→
     6→// Maps GalleryFilterState keys to reducer action types
     7→const FILTER_ACTIONS = {
     8→  mediaType: 'SET_MEDIA_TYPE',
     9→  shotFilter: 'SET_SHOT_FILTER',
    10→  excludePositioned: 'SET_EXCLUDE_POSITIONED',
    11→  searchTerm: 'SET_SEARCH_TERM',
    12→  starredOnly: 'SET_STARRED_ONLY',
    13→  toolTypeFilter: 'SET_TOOL_TYPE_FILTER',
    14→} as const;
    15→
    16→interface InternalFiltersState {
    17→  mediaType: 'all' | 'image' | 'video';
    18→  shotFilter: string;
    19→  excludePositioned: boolean;
    20→  starredOnly: boolean;
    21→  toolTypeFilter: boolean;
    22→  searchTerm: string;
    23→  isSearchOpen: boolean;
    24→}
    25→
    26→type InternalFiltersAction =
    27→  | { type: 'SET_MEDIA_TYPE'; payload: 'all' | 'image' | 'video' }
    28→  | { type: 'SET_SHOT_FILTER'; payload: string }
    29→  | { type: 'SET_EXCLUDE_POSITIONED'; payload: boolean }
    30→  | { type: 'SET_STARRED_ONLY'; payload: boolean }
    31→  | { type: 'SET_TOOL_TYPE_FILTER'; payload: boolean }
    32→  | { type: 'SET_SEARCH_TERM'; payload: string }
    33→  | { type: 'SET_IS_SEARCH_OPEN'; payload: boolean }
    34→  | { type: 'CLEAR_SEARCH' };
    35→
    36→const internalReducer = (
    37→  state: InternalFiltersState,
    38→  action: InternalFiltersAction
    39→): InternalFiltersState => {
    40→  switch (action.type) {
    41→    case 'SET_MEDIA_TYPE':
    42→      return { ...state, mediaType: action.payload };
    43→    case 'SET_SHOT_FILTER':
    44→      return { ...state, shotFilter: action.payload };
    45→    case 'SET_EXCLUDE_POSITIONED':
    46→      return { ...state, excludePositioned: action.payload };
    47→    case 'SET_STARRED_ONLY':
    48→      return { ...state, starredOnly: action.payload };
    49→    case 'SET_TOOL_TYPE_FILTER':
    50→      return { ...state, toolTypeFilter: action.payload };
    51→    case 'SET_SEARCH_TERM':
    52→      return {
    53→        ...state,
    54→        searchTerm: action.payload,
    55→        isSearchOpen: state.isSearchOpen || !!action.payload,
    56→      };
    57→    case 'SET_IS_SEARCH_OPEN':
    58→      return { ...state, isSearchOpen: action.payload };
    59→    case 'CLEAR_SEARCH':
    60→      return { ...state, searchTerm: '', isSearchOpen: false };
    61→    default:
    62→      return state;
    63→  }
    64→};
    65→
    66→interface UseMediaGalleryFiltersOptimizedProps {
    67→  images: GeneratedImageWithMetadata[];
    68→  optimisticDeletedIds: Set<string>;
    69→  currentToolType?: string;
    70→  initialFilterState?: boolean;
    71→  onServerPageChange?: (page: number, fromBottom?: boolean) => void;
    72→  serverPage?: number;
    73→  filters?: GalleryFilterState;
    74→  onFiltersChange?: (filters: GalleryFilterState) => void;
    75→  defaultFilters?: Partial<GalleryFilterState>;
    76→}
    77→
    78→export const useMediaGalleryFiltersOptimized = ({
    79→  images,
    80→  optimisticDeletedIds,
    81→  currentToolType,
    82→  initialFilterState = true,
    83→  onServerPageChange,
    84→  serverPage,
    85→  filters: controlledFilters,
    86→  onFiltersChange,
    87→  defaultFilters,
    88→}: UseMediaGalleryFiltersOptimizedProps) => {
    89→  const isControlled = controlledFilters !== undefined;
    90→
    91→   
    92→  const mergedDefaults = useMemo((): GalleryFilterState => ({
    93→    ...DEFAULT_GALLERY_FILTERS,
    94→    ...defaultFilters,
    95→  }), [defaultFilters]);
    96→
    97→  // Reducer owns filter values (uncontrolled) and isSearchOpen (both modes)
    98→  const [internalState, dispatch] = useReducer(internalReducer, {
    99→    mediaType: mergedDefaults.mediaType,
   100→    shotFilter: mergedDefaults.shotFilter,
   101→    excludePositioned: mergedDefaults.excludePositioned,
   102→    starredOnly: mergedDefaults.starredOnly,
   103→    toolTypeFilter: mergedDefaults.toolTypeFilter,
   104→    searchTerm: mergedDefaults.searchTerm,
   105→    isSearchOpen: !!mergedDefaults.searchTerm,
   106→  });
   107→
   108→  const searchInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
   109→
   110→  // Filter values: controlled reads from props, uncontrolled reads from reducer
   111→  const currentFilters: GalleryFilterState = isControlled
   112→    ? controlledFilters
   113→    : {
   114→        mediaType: internalState.mediaType,
   115→        shotFilter: internalState.shotFilter,
   116→        excludePositioned: internalState.excludePositioned,
   117→        searchTerm: internalState.searchTerm,
   118→        starredOnly: internalState.starredOnly,
   119→        toolTypeFilter: internalState.toolTypeFilter,
   120→      };
   121→
   122→  // isSearchOpen is always internal — it's UI chrome, not filter state
   123→  const isSearchOpen = internalState.isSearchOpen;
   124→
   125→  // Controlled mode calls onFiltersChange, uncontrolled dispatches to reducer
   126→  const updateFilter = useCallback(<K extends keyof GalleryFilterState>(
   127→    key: K, value: GalleryFilterState[K]
   128→  ) => {
   129→    if (isControlled) {
   130→      onFiltersChange?.({ ...controlledFilters!, [key]: value });
   131→    } else {
   132→      dispatch({ type: FILTER_ACTIONS[key], payload: value } as InternalFiltersAction);
   133→    }
   134→  }, [isControlled, controlledFilters, onFiltersChange]);
   135→
   136→  const setMediaTypeFilter = useCallback((v: 'all' | 'image' | 'video') => updateFilter('mediaType', v), [updateFilter]);
   137→  const setShotFilter = useCallback((v: string) => updateFilter('shotFilter', v), [updateFilter]);
   138→  const setExcludePositioned = useCallback((v: boolean) => updateFilter('excludePositioned', v), [updateFilter]);
   139→  const setShowStarredOnly = useCallback((v: boolean) => updateFilter('starredOnly', v), [updateFilter]);
   140→  const setToolTypeFilterEnabled = useCallback((v: boolean) => updateFilter('toolTypeFilter', v), [updateFilter]);
   141→
   142→  const setSearchTerm = useCallback((v: string) => {
   143→    updateFilter('searchTerm', v);
   144→    // Auto-open search UI when setting a non-empty term in controlled mode
   145→    // (uncontrolled mode handles this in the SET_SEARCH_TERM reducer case)
   146→    if (isControlled && v) dispatch({ type: 'SET_IS_SEARCH_OPEN', payload: true });
   147→  }, [updateFilter, isControlled]);
   148→
   149→  const setIsSearchOpen = useCallback((v: boolean) => {
   150→    dispatch({ type: 'SET_IS_SEARCH_OPEN', payload: v });
   151→  }, []);
   152→
   153→  const toggleSearch = useCallback(() => {
   154→    const opening = !internalState.isSearchOpen;
   155→    dispatch({ type: 'SET_IS_SEARCH_OPEN', payload: opening });
   156→  }, [internalState.isSearchOpen]);
   157→
   158→  useEffect(() => {
   159→    if (isSearchOpen) {
   160→      searchInputRef.current?.focus();
   161→    }
   162→  }, [isSearchOpen]);
   163→
   164→  const clearSearch = useCallback(() => {
   165→    if (isControlled) {
   166→      onFiltersChange?.({ ...controlledFilters!, searchTerm: '' });
   167→    }
   168→    dispatch({ type: 'CLEAR_SEARCH' });
   169→  }, [isControlled, controlledFilters, onFiltersChange]);
   170→
   171→  const filteredImages = useMemo(() => {
   172→    let currentFiltered = images;
   173→
   174→    currentFiltered = currentFiltered.filter(image => !optimisticDeletedIds.has(image.id));
   175→
   176→    // Tool type filter (client pagination only)
   177→    const isServerPagination = !!(onServerPageChange && serverPage);
   178→    if (!isServerPagination && initialFilterState && currentFilters.toolTypeFilter && currentToolType) {
   179→      currentFiltered = currentFiltered.filter(image => {
   180→        const metadata = image.metadata;
   181→        if (!metadata || !metadata.tool_type) return false;
   182→        if (metadata.tool_type === currentToolType) return true;
   183→        if (metadata.tool_type === `${currentToolType}-reconstructed-client`) return true;
   184→        return false;
   185→      });
   186→    }
   187→
   188→    if (!isServerPagination && currentFilters.mediaType !== 'all') {
   189→      currentFiltered = currentFiltered.filter(image => {
   190→        const isActuallyVideo = typeof image.isVideo === 'boolean' ? image.isVideo : hasVideoExtension(image.url);
   191→        if (currentFilters.mediaType === 'image') return !isActuallyVideo;
   192→        if (currentFilters.mediaType === 'video') return isActuallyVideo;
   193→        return true;
   194→      });
   195→    }
   196→
   197→    if (!isServerPagination && currentFilters.starredOnly) {
   198→      currentFiltered = currentFiltered.filter(image => image.starred === true);
   199→    }
   200→
   201→    if (!isServerPagination && currentFilters.searchTerm.trim()) {
   202→      currentFiltered = currentFiltered.filter(image => {
   203→        const prompt = image.prompt ||
   204→                      image.metadata?.prompt ||
   205→                      image.metadata?.originalParams?.orchestrator_details?.prompt ||
   206→                      '';
   207→        return prompt.toLowerCase().includes(currentFilters.searchTerm.toLowerCase());
   208→      });
   209→    }
   210→
   211→    return currentFiltered;
   212→  }, [
   213→    images,
   214→    optimisticDeletedIds,
   215→    initialFilterState,
   216→    currentFilters.toolTypeFilter,
   217→    currentFilters.mediaType,
   218→    currentFilters.starredOnly,
   219→    currentFilters.searchTerm,
   220→    currentToolType,
   221→    onServerPageChange,
   222→    serverPage,
   223→  ]);
   224→
   225→  return {
   226→    filterByToolType: initialFilterState,
   227→    mediaTypeFilter: currentFilters.mediaType,
   228→    shotFilter: currentFilters.shotFilter,
   229→    excludePositioned: currentFilters.excludePositioned,
   230→    showStarredOnly: currentFilters.starredOnly,
   231→    toolTypeFilterEnabled: currentFilters.toolTypeFilter,
   232→    searchTerm: currentFilters.searchTerm,
   233→    isSearchOpen,
   234→    searchInputRef,
   235→
   236→    setMediaTypeFilter,
   237→    setShotFilter,
   238→    setExcludePositioned,
   239→    setShowStarredOnly,
   240→    setToolTypeFilterEnabled,
   241→    setSearchTerm,
   242→    setIsSearchOpen,
   243→
   244→    filteredImages,
   245→    toggleSearch,
   246→    clearSearch,
   247→  };
   248→};
   249→

    34→
    35→const MIN_PADDING = 60;
    36→const MAX_PADDING = 200;
    37→
    38→function clampPadding(value: number): number {
    39→  return Math.min(Math.max(value, MIN_PADDING), MAX_PADDING);
    40→}
    41→
    42→function extractResolutionFromMetadata(image: GeneratedImageWithMetadata): { width?: number; height?: number } {
    43→  let width = image.metadata?.width;
    44→  let height = image.metadata?.height;
    45→
    46→  if (width && height) {
    47→    return { width, height };
    48→  }
    49→
    50→  const resolution = image.metadata?.originalParams?.orchestrator_details?.resolution;
    51→  if (resolution && typeof resolution === 'string' && resolution.includes('x')) {
    52→    const [parsedWidth, parsedHeight] = resolution.split('x').map(Number);
    53→    if (!isNaN(parsedWidth) && !isNaN(parsedHeight)) {
    54→      width = parsedWidth;
    55→      height = parsedHeight;
    56→    }
    57→  }
    58→
    59→  return { width, height };
    60→}
    61→
    62→function resolveAspectRatioPadding(
    63→  image: GeneratedImageWithMetadata,
    64→  projectAspectRatio?: string,
    65→): string {
    66→  if (projectAspectRatio) {
    67→    const ratio = parseRatio(projectAspectRatio);
    68→    if (!isNaN(ratio)) {
    69→      const calculatedPadding = (1 / ratio) * 100;
    70→      return `${clampPadding(calculatedPadding)}%`;
    71→    }
    72→  }
    73→
    74→  const { width, height } = extractResolutionFromMetadata(image);
    75→  if (width && height) {
    76→    return `${clampPadding((height / width) * 100)}%`;
    77→  }
    78→
    79→  return '100%';
    80→}

   234→  const { addToShot, addToShotWithoutPosition } = useMediaGalleryItemShotActions({
   235→    imageId: image.id,

   239→    displayUrl: displayUrl || image.url,
   240→    selectedShotId: selectedShotIdLocal,

   250→  });
   251→  const shouldShowMetadata = useMemo(() => {

   256→  }, [image.metadata, isMobile, mobilePopoverOpenImageId, image.id, isInfoOpen]);
   257→  const isCurrentDeleting = isDeleting === true || isDeleting === image.id;
   258→  const imageKey = image.id || `image-${actualDisplayUrl}-${index}`;
   259→  const isPlaceholder = !image.id && actualDisplayUrl === "/placeholder.svg";
   260→  const currentTargetShotName = selectedShotIdLocal ? simplifiedShotOptions.find(s => s.id === selectedShotIdLocal)?.name : undefined;
   261→  const handleDragStart = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {

   316→      </div>

   318→  }

   413→                showTickForImageId,

   437→            hideOnHover={!isVideoContent}

   443→            isMobile={isMobile}

   449→            showShare={showShare}

   451→            handleShare={handleShare}
   452→            isCreatingShare={isCreatingShare}

   456→          />
   457→          <ActionButtons

   459→            localStarred={localStarred}
   460→            isTogglingStar={isTogglingStar}
   461→            isDeleting={isCurrentDeleting}
   462→            showStar={showStar}
   463→            showEdit={showEdit}
   464→            showDelete={showDelete}
   465→            onToggleStar={onToggleStar}
   466→            setIsTogglingStar={setIsTogglingStar}
   467→            setLocalStarred={setLocalStarred}
   468→            onOpenLightbox={onOpenLightbox}
   469→            onDelete={onDelete}
   470→          />
   471→      </div>)
   472→      }
   473→    </div>
   474→  );
   475→  return isMobile ? (

   477→      {imageContent}

   497→          projectId={selectedProjectId ?? undefined}

    31→
    32→export function useImageGenGallery({
    33→  projectId,
    34→  effectiveProjectId,

    36→  formAssociatedShotId,

    43→}: UseImageGenGalleryParams) {
    44→  const [galleryFilters, setGalleryFilters] = useState<GalleryFilterState>({
    45→    ...DEFAULT_GALLERY_FILTERS,
    46→    mediaType: 'image',
    47→    toolTypeFilter: false,
    48→  });
    49→
    50→  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);
    51→  const [lastKnownTotal, setLastKnownTotal] = useState<number>(0);
    52→  const [isPageChange, setIsPageChange] = useState(false);
    53→  const [isPageChangeFromBottom, setIsPageChangeFromBottom] = useState(false);
    54→  const [isFilterChange, setIsFilterChange] = useState(false);
    55→  const scrollPosRef = useRef<number>(0);
    56→
    57→  const pagePrefs = useAutoSaveSettings<ImageGenPagePrefs>({

    61→    scope: 'shot',
    62→    defaults: EMPTY_PAGE_PREFS,
    63→    enabled: !!formAssociatedShotId && !!projectId,

    65→
    66→  const [galleryRef, containerDimensions] = useContainerDimensions(150, isPhoneOnly);
    67→
    68→  const galleryLayout = useMemo(() => {
    69→    return calculateGalleryLayout(
    70→      projectAspectRatio,
    71→      isMobile,
    72→      containerDimensions.width,
    73→      containerDimensions.height,
    74→      true // reducedSpacing
    75→    );
    76→  }, [projectAspectRatio, isMobile, containerDimensions.width, containerDimensions.height]);
    77→
    78→  // Locked-in skeleton layout — calculated once from window dimensions to prevent jitter
    79→  const skeletonLayoutRef = useRef<{ columns: number; itemsPerPage: number } | null>(null);
    80→  if (skeletonLayoutRef.current === null) {
    81→    const estimatedWidth = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? Math.floor(window.innerWidth * 0.9) : 800;
    82→    const estimatedHeight = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.innerHeight - 150 : 600;
    83→    const stableLayout = calculateGalleryLayout(projectAspectRatio, isMobile, estimatedWidth, estimatedHeight, true);
    84→    skeletonLayoutRef.current = {
    85→      columns: stableLayout.columns,
    86→      itemsPerPage: stableLayout.itemsPerPage,

   102→  }), [galleryFilters]);
   103→

   105→    effectiveProjectId,

   111→
   112→  const imagesToShow = useMemo(() => {

   138→    if (lastAppliedPagePrefsForShotRef.current === formAssociatedShotId) return;
   139→    lastAppliedPagePrefsForShotRef.current = formAssociatedShotId;

   144→    } else {
   145→      setGalleryFilters(prev => ({ ...prev, shotFilter: formAssociatedShotId }));
   146→    }
   147→  }, [formAssociatedShotId, pagePrefs.status, pagePrefs.settings.galleryFilterOverride]);
   148→
   149→  useEffect(() => {
   150→    if (searchParams.get('scrollToGallery') === 'true') {
   151→      const checkAndScroll = () => {
   152→        if (galleryRef.current && !isLoadingGenerations) {
   153→          if (!isFormExpanded && galleryRef.current) {
   154→            galleryRef.current.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
   155→          } else if (formContainerRef.current) {
   156→            formContainerRef.current.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
   157→          }
   158→        } else {
   159→          setTimeout(checkAndScroll, 100);
   160→        }
   161→      };
   162→      setTimeout(checkAndScroll, 150);
   163→    }
   164→  }, [searchParams, generationsResponse, isLoadingGenerations, isFormExpanded, galleryRef, formContainerRef]);
   165→
   166→  // Scroll restoration: top-of-gallery for "from bottom" nav, saved position otherwise
   167→  useEffect(() => {
   168→    if (generationsResponse && isPageChange) {

   170→        if (galleryRef.current) {
   171→          const rect = galleryRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
   172→          const scrollTop = window.pageYOffset || document.documentElement.scrollTop;
   173→          const targetPosition = rect.top + scrollTop - (isMobile ? 80 : 20);
   174→          window.scrollTo({
   175→            top: Math.max(0, targetPosition),
   176→            behavior: 'smooth',
   177→          });
   178→        }
   179→      } else {
   180→        window.scrollTo({ top: scrollPosRef.current, behavior: 'auto' });
   181→      }
   182→      setIsPageChange(false);
   183→      setIsPageChangeFromBottom(false);
   184→    }
   185→  }, [generationsResponse, isPageChange, isPageChangeFromBottom, galleryRef, isMobile]);
   186→
   187→  const isSticky = useStickyHeader({
   188→    containerRef: collapsibleContainerRef,
   189→    isMobile,
   190→    isFormExpanded,
   191→  });
   192→
   193→  const handleServerPageChange = useCallback((page: number, fromBottom?: boolean) => {
   194→    if (!fromBottom) {
   195→      scrollPosRef.current = window.scrollY;

   197→    setIsPageChange(true);

   199→    setCurrentPage(page);
   200→  }, []);
   201→
   202→  const totalCount = generationsResponse?.total ?? lastKnownTotal;
   203→  const totalPages = Math.ceil(totalCount / itemsPerPage);
   204→  // Arrow-key page navigation. Skips when an input is focused or a dialog is open.
   205→  useEffect(() => {

   207→      if (e.key !== 'ArrowLeft' && e.key !== 'ArrowRight') return;
   208→
   209→      const target = e.target as HTMLElement;
   210→      const tag = target?.tagName;
   211→      const isInput = tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA' || target?.isContentEditable;
   212→      const dialog = document.querySelector('[role="dialog"], [data-state="open"].fixed');
   213→
   214→      if (isInput || dialog) return;
   215→
   216→      if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' && currentPage > 1) {
   217→        e.preventDefault();
   218→        handleServerPageChange(currentPage - 1);
   219→      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' && currentPage < totalPages) {
   220→        e.preventDefault();
   221→        handleServerPageChange(currentPage + 1);
   222→      }
   223→    };
   224→
   225→    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
   226→    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
   227→  }, [currentPage, totalPages, handleServerPageChange]);
   228→
   229→  const handleGalleryFiltersChange = useCallback((newFilters: GalleryFilterState) => {
   230→    if (newFilters.shotFilter !== galleryFilters.shotFilter) {
   231→      if (formAssociatedShotId && pagePrefs.status === 'ready') {
   232→        const shouldSaveOverride = newFilters.shotFilter !== formAssociatedShotId;
   233→        const valueToSave = shouldSaveOverride ? newFilters.shotFilter : undefined;
   234→        pagePrefs.updateField('galleryFilterOverride', valueToSave);
   235→      }
   236→    }
   237→    setGalleryFilters(newFilters);
   238→  }, [galleryFilters.shotFilter, formAssociatedShotId, pagePrefs]);
   239→
   240→  const handleSwitchToAssociatedShot = useCallback((shotId: string) => {
   241→    setGalleryFilters(prev => ({ ...prev, shotFilter: shotId }));
   242→    if (formAssociatedShotId && pagePrefs.status === 'ready') {
   243→      const shouldSaveOverride = shotId !== formAssociatedShotId;
   244→      const valueToSave = shouldSaveOverride ? shotId : undefined;
   245→      pagePrefs.updateField('galleryFilterOverride', valueToSave);
   246→    }
   247→  }, [formAssociatedShotId, pagePrefs]);
   248→
   249→  return {
   250→    galleryFilters,
   251→    setGalleryFilters,
   252→    currentPage,
   253→    itemsPerPage,
   254→    lastKnownTotal,
   255→    isFilterChange,
   256→    generationsResponse,
   257→    generationsFilters,
   258→    isLoadingGenerations,
   259→    isPlaceholderData,
   260→    imagesToShow,
   261→    galleryRef,

   264→    isSticky,
   265→    handleServerPageChange,
   266→    handleGalleryFiltersChange,
   267→    handleSwitchToAssociatedShot,
   268→  };
   269→}

1→import { useCallback } from "react";
     2→import { toast } from '@/shared/components/ui/runtime/sonner';
     3→import type { AddToShotHandler } from '@/shared/types/imageHandlers';
     4→
     5→/**
     6→ * Check if an error is retryable (network-related but not auth/server errors)
     7→ * @internal - only used within this file
     8→ */
     9→function isRetryableNetworkError(err: unknown): boolean {
    10→  const message = (err as Error)?.message?.toLowerCase() || '';
    11→  const status = (err instanceof Object && 'status' in err) ? (err as { status: unknown }).status : undefined;
    12→
    13→  const isNetworkError = message.includes('load failed') ||
    14→    message.includes('network error') ||
    15→    message.includes('fetch') ||
    16→    message.includes('timeout');
    17→
    18→  const isServerError = message.includes('unauthorized') ||
    19→    message.includes('forbidden') ||
    20→    message.includes('not found') ||
    21→    message.includes('quota') ||
    22→    status === 401 ||
    23→    status === 403 ||
    24→    status === 404;
    25→
    26→  return isNetworkError && !isServerError;
    27→}
    28→
    29→interface UseAddToShotWithRetryOptions {
    30→  generationId: string;
    31→  imageUrl: string;
    32→  thumbUrl: string;
    33→  displayUrl: string;
    34→  selectedShotId: string;
    35→  isMobile: boolean;
    36→  onAddToShot: AddToShotHandler;
    37→  onSuccess: () => void;
    38→  onStartLoading: () => void;
    39→  onEndLoading: () => void;
    40→  errorMessage: string;
    41→}
    42→
    43→/**
    44→ * Execute an add-to-shot operation with retry logic for network failures
    45→ * @internal - only used within this file
    46→ */
    47→async function executeWithRetry(
    48→  options: UseAddToShotWithRetryOptions,
    49→  toast: typeof toast
    50→): Promise<void> {
    51→  const {
    52→    generationId,
    53→    imageUrl,
    54→    thumbUrl,
    55→    displayUrl,
    56→    selectedShotId,
    57→    isMobile,
    58→    onAddToShot,
    59→    onSuccess,
    60→    onStartLoading,
    61→    onEndLoading,
    62→    errorMessage,
    63→  } = options;
    64→
    65→  onStartLoading();
    66→
    67→  try {
    68→    let success = false;
    69→    let retryCount = 0;
    70→    const maxRetries = isMobile ? 2 : 1;
    71→
    72→    while (!success && retryCount < maxRetries) {
    73→      try {
    74→        const imageUrlToUse = imageUrl || displayUrl;
    75→        const thumbUrlToUse = thumbUrl || imageUrlToUse;
    76→
    77→        success = await onAddToShot(selectedShotId, generationId, imageUrlToUse, thumbUrlToUse);
    78→
    79→        if (success) {
    80→          onSuccess();
    81→        }
    82→      } catch (error) {
    83→        retryCount++;
    84→
    85→        if (retryCount < maxRetries && isRetryableNetworkError(error)) {
    86→          if (retryCount === 1) {
    87→            toast({
    88→              title: "Retrying...",
    89→              description: "Network issue detected, trying again.",
    90→            });
    91→          }
    92→
    93→          const waitTime = 800;
    94→          await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, waitTime));
    95→        } else {
    96→          const errorDesc = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error';
    97→          toast({
    98→            title: "Network Error",
    99→            description: `${errorMessage} ${isMobile ? 'Please check your connection and try again.' : errorDesc}`,
   100→            variant: "destructive"
   101→          });
   102→          throw error;
   103→        }
   104→      }
   105→    }
   106→  } finally {
   107→    onEndLoading();
   108→  }
   109→}
   110→
   111→interface UseShotActionsProps {
   112→  imageId: string;
   113→  generationId: string;
   114→  imageUrl: string;
   115→  thumbUrl: string;
   116→  displayUrl: string;
   117→  selectedShotId: string;
   118→  isMobile: boolean;
   119→  onAddToLastShot?: AddToShotHandler;
   120→  onAddToLastShotWithoutPosition?: AddToShotHandler;
   121→  onShowTick: (id: string) => void;
   122→  onShowSecondaryTick?: (id: string) => void;
   123→  onOptimisticPositioned?: (id: string, shotId: string) => void;
   124→  onOptimisticUnpositioned?: (id: string, shotId: string) => void;
   125→  setAddingToShotImageId: (id: string | null) => void;
   126→  setAddingToShotWithoutPositionImageId?: (id: string | null) => void;
   127→}
   128→
   129→/**
   130→ * Hook to manage add-to-shot actions with retry logic
   131→ */
   132→export function useMediaGalleryItemShotActions(props: UseShotActionsProps) {
   133→  const {
   134→    imageId,
   135→    generationId,
   136→    imageUrl,
   137→    thumbUrl,
   138→    displayUrl,
   139→    selectedShotId,
   140→    isMobile,
   141→    onAddToLastShot,
   142→    onAddToLastShotWithoutPosition,
   143→    onShowTick,
   144→    onShowSecondaryTick,
   145→    onOptimisticPositioned,
   146→    onOptimisticUnpositioned,
   147→    setAddingToShotImageId,
   148→    setAddingToShotWithoutPositionImageId,
   149→  } = props;
   150→
   151→  const addToShot = useCallback(async () => {
   152→    if (!onAddToLastShot) {
   153→      return;
   154→    }
   155→
   156→    if (!selectedShotId) {
   157→      toast({
   158→        title: "Select a Shot",
   159→        description: "Please select a shot first to add this image.",
   160→        variant: "destructive"
   161→      });
   162→      return;
   163→    }
   164→
   165→    await executeWithRetry({
   166→      generationId,
   167→      imageUrl,
   168→      thumbUrl,
   169→      displayUrl,
   170→      selectedShotId,
   171→      isMobile,
   172→      onAddToShot: onAddToLastShot,
   173→      onSuccess: () => {
   174→        onShowTick(imageId);
   175→        onOptimisticPositioned?.(imageId, selectedShotId);
   176→      },
   177→      onStartLoading: () => setAddingToShotImageId(imageId),
   178→      onEndLoading: () => setAddingToShotImageId(null),
   179→      errorMessage: "Could not add image to shot.",
   180→    }, toast);
   181→  }, [
   182→    imageId, generationId, imageUrl, thumbUrl, displayUrl, selectedShotId, isMobile,
   183→    onAddToLastShot, onShowTick, onOptimisticPositioned,
   184→    setAddingToShotImageId
   185→  ]);
   186→
   187→  const addToShotWithoutPosition = useCallback(async () => {
   188→    if (!onAddToLastShotWithoutPosition || !setAddingToShotWithoutPositionImageId) {
   189→      return;
   190→    }
   191→
   192→    await executeWithRetry({
   193→      generationId,
   194→      imageUrl,
   195→      thumbUrl,
   196→      displayUrl,
   197→      selectedShotId,
   198→      isMobile,
   199→      onAddToShot: onAddToLastShotWithoutPosition,
   200→      onSuccess: () => {
   201→        onShowSecondaryTick?.(imageId);
   202→        onOptimisticUnpositioned?.(imageId, selectedShotId);
   203→      },
   204→      onStartLoading: () => setAddingToShotWithoutPositionImageId(imageId),
   205→      onEndLoading: () => setAddingToShotWithoutPositionImageId(null),
   206→      errorMessage: "Could not add image to shot without position.",
   207→    }, toast);
   208→  }, [
   209→    imageId, generationId, imageUrl, thumbUrl, displayUrl, selectedShotId, isMobile,
   210→    onAddToLastShotWithoutPosition, onShowSecondaryTick, onOptimisticUnpositioned,
   211→    setAddingToShotWithoutPositionImageId
   212→  ]);
   213→
   214→  return {
   215→    addToShot,
   216→    addToShotWithoutPosition,
   217→  };
   218→}
   219→

1→import { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useMemo, useRef } from "react";
     2→import { getDisplayUrl, stripQueryParameters } from "@/shared/lib/media/mediaUrl";
     3→import { hasLoadedImage, setImageLoadStatus } from "@/shared/lib/preloading";
     4→import type { GeneratedImageWithMetadata } from "../../MediaGallery/types";
     5→
     6→interface UseImageLoadingProps {
     7→  image: GeneratedImageWithMetadata;
     8→  displayUrl: string;
     9→  shouldLoad: boolean;
    10→  onImageLoaded?: (id: string) => void;
    11→}
    12→
    13→interface UseImageLoadingReturn {
    14→  actualSrc: string | null;
    15→  actualDisplayUrl: string;
    16→  imageLoaded: boolean;
    17→  imageLoading: boolean;
    18→  imageLoadError: boolean;
    19→  handleImageLoad: () => void;
    20→  handleImageError: (e?: React.SyntheticEvent<Element>) => void;
    21→  retryImageLoad: () => void;
    22→  setImageLoading: (loading: boolean) => void;
    23→}
    24→
    25→const MAX_RETRIES = 2;
    26→
    27→export function useImageLoading({
    28→  image,
    29→  displayUrl,
    30→  shouldLoad,
    31→  onImageLoaded,
    32→}: UseImageLoadingProps): UseImageLoadingReturn {
    33→  const isPreloadedAndCached = hasLoadedImage(image);
    34→
    35→  const [imageLoadError, setImageLoadError] = useState<boolean>(false);
    36→  const [imageRetryCount, setImageRetryCount] = useState<number>(0);
    37→  const [imageLoaded, setImageLoaded] = useState<boolean>(isPreloadedAndCached);
    38→  const [imageLoading, setImageLoading] = useState<boolean>(false);
    39→  const [actualSrc, setActualSrc] = useState<string | null>(null);
    40→  const [scheduledRetryAttempt, setScheduledRetryAttempt] = useState<number | null>(null);
    41→
    42→  const retryDelayTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
    43→  const retryApplyTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
    44→
    45→  const imageIdentifier = useMemo(() => {
    46→    return `${image.id}:${image.urlIdentity || image.url || ''}:${image.thumbUrlIdentity || image.thumbUrl || ''}:${image.updatedAt || ''}`;
    47→  }, [image.id, image.urlIdentity, image.url, image.thumbUrlIdentity, image.thumbUrl, image.updatedAt]);
    48→
    49→  const prevImageIdentifierRef = useRef<string>(imageIdentifier);
    50→
    51→  const clearRetryTimers = useCallback(() => {
    52→    if (retryDelayTimerRef.current) {
    53→      clearTimeout(retryDelayTimerRef.current);
    54→      retryDelayTimerRef.current = null;
    55→    }
    56→    if (retryApplyTimerRef.current) {
    57→      clearTimeout(retryApplyTimerRef.current);
    58→      retryApplyTimerRef.current = null;
    59→    }
    60→  }, []);
    61→
    62→  const actualDisplayUrl = useMemo(() => {
    63→    if (imageRetryCount > 0) {
    64→      return getDisplayUrl(image.thumbUrl || image.url, true);
    65→    }
    66→    return displayUrl;
    67→  }, [displayUrl, image.thumbUrl, image.url, imageRetryCount]);
    68→
    69→  const handleImageLoad = useCallback(() => {
    70→    setImageLoaded(true);
    71→    setImageLoading(false);
    72→    try {
    73→      setImageLoadStatus(image, true);
    74→    } catch {
    75→      // cache writes are a best-effort optimization
    76→    }
    77→    onImageLoaded?.(image.id);
    78→  }, [image, onImageLoaded]);
    79→
    80→  const handleImageError = useCallback((errorEvent?: React.SyntheticEvent<Element>) => {
    81→    const failedSrc = (errorEvent?.target as HTMLImageElement | HTMLVideoElement)?.src || displayUrl;
    82→
    83→    setImageLoading(false);
    84→
    85→    if (failedSrc?.includes('/placeholder.svg') || failedSrc?.includes('undefined') || !failedSrc) {
    86→      clearRetryTimers();
    87→      setScheduledRetryAttempt(null);
    88→      setImageLoadError(true);
    89→      return;
    90→    }
    91→
    92→    if (imageRetryCount < MAX_RETRIES) {
    93→      setImageLoadError(false);
    94→      setScheduledRetryAttempt(imageRetryCount + 1);
    95→      return;
    96→    }
    97→
    98→    clearRetryTimers();
    99→    setScheduledRetryAttempt(null);
   100→    setImageLoadError(true);
   101→  }, [clearRetryTimers, displayUrl, imageRetryCount]);
   102→
   103→  const retryImageLoad = useCallback(() => {
   104→    clearRetryTimers();
   105→    setImageLoadError(false);
   106→    setImageRetryCount(0);
   107→    setScheduledRetryAttempt(null);
   108→    setActualSrc(null);
   109→    setImageLoaded(false);
   110→    setImageLoading(false);
   111→  }, [clearRetryTimers]);
   112→
   113→  useEffect(() => {
   114→    if (prevImageIdentifierRef.current === imageIdentifier) {
   115→      return;
   116→    }
   117→
   118→    clearRetryTimers();
   119→    prevImageIdentifierRef.current = imageIdentifier;
   120→
   121→    setImageLoadError(false);
   122→    setImageRetryCount(0);
   123→    setScheduledRetryAttempt(null);
   124→    const isNewImageCached = hasLoadedImage(image);
   125→    setImageLoaded(isNewImageCached);
   126→    if (!isNewImageCached) {
   127→      setImageLoading(false);
   128→    }
   129→    setActualSrc(null);
   130→  }, [clearRetryTimers, imageIdentifier, image]);
   131→
   132→  useEffect(() => {
   133→    if (scheduledRetryAttempt === null) {
   134→      return;
   135→    }
   136→
   137→    clearRetryTimers();
   138→
   139→    retryDelayTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
   140→      setImageRetryCount(scheduledRetryAttempt);
   141→      setActualSrc(null);
   142→
   143→      retryApplyTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
   144→        setActualSrc(getDisplayUrl(image.thumbUrl || image.url, true));
   145→        setScheduledRetryAttempt(null);
   146→      }, 100);
   147→    }, 1000 * scheduledRetryAttempt);
   148→
   149→    return clearRetryTimers;
   150→  }, [clearRetryTimers, image.thumbUrl, image.url, scheduledRetryAttempt]);
   151→
   152→  useEffect(() => clearRetryTimers, [clearRetryTimers]);
   153→
   154→  useEffect(() => {
   155→    const isPreloaded = hasLoadedImage(image);
   156→
   157→    if (shouldLoad && actualDisplayUrl) {
   158→      if (actualDisplayUrl === '/placeholder.svg') {
   159→        setImageLoadError(true);
   160→        return;
   161→      }
   162→
   163→      const isActuallyDifferent = actualSrc !== actualDisplayUrl;
   164→      const isDifferentFile = stripQueryParameters(actualSrc) !== stripQueryParameters(actualDisplayUrl);
   165→
   166→      if (isActuallyDifferent && isDifferentFile) {
   167→        if (!isPreloaded && !actualSrc) {
   168→          setImageLoading(true);
   169→        }
   170→
   171→        setActualSrc(actualDisplayUrl);
   172→      }
   173→    }
   174→  }, [actualSrc, actualDisplayUrl, shouldLoad, image]);
   175→
   176→  return {
   177→    actualSrc,
   178→    actualDisplayUrl,
   179→    imageLoaded,
   180→    imageLoading,
   181→    imageLoadError,
   182→    handleImageLoad,
   183→    handleImageError,
   184→    retryImageLoad,
   185→    setImageLoading,
   186→  };
   187→}
   188→

    34→    onDelete,
    35→    onToggleStar,
    36→  } = props;
    37→
    38→  const hasValidData = !!(data?.items && data.items.length > 0);
    39→  const isLoadingOrFetching = loading || fetching;
    40→  const shouldShowSkeleton = (isLoadingOrFetching || videosViewJustEnabled) && hasValidData;
    41→
    42→  if (shouldShowSkeleton && data?.items) {
    43→    return (
    44→      <div className="space-y-4 pt-4 border-t">
    45→        <h2 className="text-xl font-medium">Previous Results ({data.items.length})</h2>
    46→        <SkeletonGallery
    47→          count={data.items.length}
    48→          columns={SKELETON_COLUMNS[3]}
    49→          showControls={true}
    50→          projectAspectRatio={projectAspectRatio}
    51→        />
    52→      </div>
    53→    );
    54→  }
    55→
    56→  if (hasValidData && data?.items) {
    57→    const itemsPerPage = getVideoGalleryItemsPerPage(isMobile);
    58→    return (
    59→      <div className="space-y-4 pt-4 border-t">
    60→        <h2 className="text-xl font-medium">Previous Results ({data.items.length})</h2>
    61→        <MediaGallery
    62→          images={data.items}
    63→          allShots={[]}
    64→          onAddToLastShot={async () => false}
    65→          onAddToLastShotWithoutPosition={async () => false}
    66→          onDelete={onDelete}
    67→          onToggleStar={onToggleStar}
    68→          isDeleting={deletingId}
    69→          currentToolType={TOOL_IDS.CHARACTER_ANIMATE}
    70→          defaultFilters={VIDEO_ONLY_GALLERY_FILTERS}
    71→          currentToolTypeName="Animate Characters"
    72→          columnsPerRow={3}
    73→          pagination={{ itemsPerPage }}
    74→          config={buildVideoResultsGalleryConfig(data.items.length, isMobile)}
    75→        />
    76→      </div>
    77→    );
    78→  }
    79→
    80→  if (!isLoadingOrFetching) {
    81→    return (
    82→      <div className="text-sm text-muted-foreground text-center pt-4 border-t">
    83→        No animations yet. Create your first one above!
    84→      </div>
    85→    );
    86→  }
    87→
    88→  return null;
    89→}
    90→

### 3. **`src/shared/components/MediaGallery/MediaGallery.tsx` — Full Contents**

This is the top-level orchestrator component (487 lines). Key structure:

**Imports & Types:**
- Context hooks: `useCurrentShot`, `useProjectCrudContext`, `useProjectSelectionContext`, `useShotAdditionSelectionOptional`
- Mobile detection: `useIsMobile`, `useIsTablet`
- All specialized hooks from the hooks directory
- Components: `MediaGalleryHeader`, `ShotNotifier`, `MediaGalleryGrid`, `MediaGalleryLightbox`, `MobileBottomBar`

**Core Logic:**
- `useAspectRatioLayout()` hook (lines 46–79): Calculates layout based on aspect ratio, responsive columns, and pagination
- **Hooks composed** (lines 170–344):
  - `useMediaGalleryState` — manages lightbox, optimistic states, UI chrome (line 170)
  - `useMediaGalleryFilters` — filter/search logic (line 178)
  - `useMediaGalleryPagination` — pagination state (line 190)

- Gallery container with header, filters, pagination
- `MediaGalleryGrid` with paginatedImages + item props
- `MediaGalleryLightbox` modal
- `MobileBottomBar` for phones only

**Key Pattern:** All child hooks receive state from parent hooks and pass callbacks down. No direct data flow from component tree — everything funnels through the hook composition chain.

### 6. **Re-export Pattern in features/gallery/**

The entire `features/gallery/components/MediaGallery/` directory is **pure re-exports** of shared/ code:

- `features/.../MediaGallery.tsx` → re-exports `shared/.../MediaGallery`
- `features/.../index.ts` → same index signature as shared (see above)
- `features/.../hooks/useContainerWidth.ts` → `export * from '@/shared/components/MediaGallery/hooks/useContainerWidth'`
- `features/.../utils/index.ts` → `export * from '@/shared/components/MediaGallery/utils'`

**No custom logic or overrides** in features/gallery. This is purely a module alias pattern for convenience imports from the features domain.

---

### 7. **Optimized vs Non-Optimized Hooks**

**Only "Optimized" versions exist** — there are no non-optimized predecessors:

- **`useMediaGalleryStateOptimized.ts`** (435 lines) — **The only state hook**
  - Uses `useReducer` for consolidated state management
  - Manages 16 different state fields via a single reducer
  - Actions include batch updates (e.g., `MARK_OPTIMISTIC_DELETED_WITH_BACKFILL` combines deletion + loading state)
  - Syncs `activeLightboxMedia` with updated images to preserve metadata like `__autoEnterEditMode`
  - Reconciliation logic to clean up optimistic state when images change

- **`useMediaGalleryFiltersOptimized.ts`** (248 lines) — **The only filters hook**
  - Supports both controlled and uncontrolled modes
  - Reducer owns filter values and `isSearchOpen` state
  - Filter logic is client-side only for non-server-pagination; server-side filtering is delegated to parent
  - Implements tool-type filtering, media-type filtering, search (case-insensitive prompt), starred-only, excludePositioned
  - `isSearchOpen` is UI-only state, separate from filter values

**The "Optimized" naming likely reflects a past refactoring** where these replaced larger, less performant versions. The code is well-optimized with proper memoization and dispatch batching.

---

### 8. **MediaGalleryItem Files**

**`src/shared/components/MediaGalleryItem.tsx`** (503 lines) — Single gallery item component

**Architecture:**
- Props destructured into: `shotWorkflow`, `mobileInteraction`, `features`, `actions`, `loading`
- Uses **6 custom hooks** for concerns:
  - `useMediaGalleryItemState` — local UI state (star, modal, info panel, shot selector)
  - `useImageLoading` — image load/error/retry logic
  - `useStableMediaUrls` — URL stability and progressive loading
  - `useShotPositionChecks` — whether image is already positioned in selected shot
  - `useItemInteraction` — tap/click handling for mobile vs desktop
  - `useMediaGalleryItemShotActions` — add-to-shot with retry logic

**Rendering Structure:**
- Aspect ratio padding calculated dynamically (via `resolveAspectRatioPadding`)
- Conditional content: `VideoContent` vs `ImageContent`
- Overlay layer (z-10) with: shot badges, shot actions, timestamp, metadata bar, action buttons
- Wrapped in `DraggableImage` on desktop, plain fragment on mobile
- Drag-drop support with `setGenerationDragData` and `createDragPreview`

**Key Details:**
- Uses `getGenerationId(media)` helper for variant handling (not inline `media.generation_id || media.id`)
- Calls `usePrefetchTaskData` on mouse enter for task details prefetch
- Supports both video and image tasks with different behavior
- Mobile touch: `handleTouchStart` + `handleInteraction` for single/double tap logic

**MediaGalleryItem hooks directory** (11 files, ~1,300 lines total):

- `useShotActions.ts` — 218 lines (add-to-shot with retry, network error handling)
- `useImageLoading.ts` — 187 lines (load states, retries up to 2x, preload cache)
- `useStableMediaUrls.ts` — 94 lines (progressive loading, video src vs thumb URL)
- `useMediaGalleryItemState.ts` — 116 lines (local open/close states for modals)
- `useShotPositionChecks.ts` — 104 lines (checks if already positioned in selected shot)
- `useItemInteraction.ts` — 66 lines (mobile tap vs desktop click routing)
- `useShotActionController.ts` — 89 lines (command pattern for shot actions)
- `useMediaGalleryItemTaskShare.ts` — 46 lines (share link generation)
- `useMediaGalleryItemShotWorkflow.ts` — 87 lines (shot workflow context)
- Tests: `useImageLoading.test.ts` (103), `useItemInteraction.test.ts` (146), `useShotActionController.test.ts` (67)

---

### Key Architectural Insights

1. **Monolithic MediaGallery, Decomposed Concerns:** The main `MediaGallery.tsx` is 487 lines but manages 10+ hooks. Complexity is hidden in hook composition, not inline code.

2. **Reducer-Based State:** Both state and filters use `useReducer` for predictable updates and batch operations (e.g., optimistic deletion + backfill loading in one action).

3. **Optimistic UI Pattern:** Heavy use of `optimisticDeletedIds`, `optimisticPositionedIds`, `optimisticUnpositionedIds` sets to track pending operations before server confirmation.

4. **Two-Layer Item Architecture:**
   - **MediaGalleryItem.tsx** — Item render + visual layout
   - **MediaGalleryItem/hooks/** — Domain-specific concerns (loading, interaction, shot operations)

5. **Re-export Strategy:** `features/gallery/` is entirely re-exports of `shared/`. No domain-specific customization; purely a module convenience layer.

6. **No Parallel "NonOptimized" Versions:** The "Optimized" suffix is historical; these are now the canonical implementations.

- hooks/useContainerWidth.ts → re-export
- utils/index.ts → re-export

**Consumers** (10 total across 5 tools + features/gallery + shared/MediaSelectionPanel):
- All tools import from `@/shared/components/MediaGallery`
- Only features/gallery imports from `@/features/gallery/components/MediaGallery` (2 files)

**`src/shared/components/MediaGalleryItem/`** — separate item component layer:
- MediaGalleryItem.tsx (503 lines) + 9 hooks + types

## Known Issues
1. **"Optimized" suffix**: `useMediaGalleryStateOptimized` and `useMediaGalleryFiltersOptimized` — no non-optimized versions exist, the suffix is vestigial
2. **Unnecessary re-export layer**: `features/gallery/components/MediaGallery/` is 4 files of pure re-exports with only 2 consumers — could those 2 consumers just import from shared directly?
3. **MediaGallery.tsx at 487 lines** with an inline `useAspectRatioLayout` function that could be extracted
4. **Hook naming verbosity**: Every hook has `useMediaGallery` prefix which is redundant given they're in the MediaGallery/hooks/ directory
5. **Utils naming**: `mediaGallery-` prefix on every util file is redundant given directory
6. **`useMediaGalleryStateOptimized` at 435 lines** — largest hook, may benefit from further decomposition
7. **types.ts at 207 lines** — mixes contracts (GalleryFilterState from contracts/), config types, and prop types

## Constraints
- CLAUDE.md: "every change should make the codebase smaller or more explicit"
- CLAUDE.md: flag components >300 LOC
- Must not break the 10 consumers
- Must keep tests passing
- Avoid over-engineering — only clean up what genuinely improves clarity

Design a practical cleanup plan. Focus on high-impact, low-risk changes. Don't propose changes just for the sake of change.

    45→
    46→function useAspectRatioLayout({
    47→  projectAspectRatio,
    48→  isMobile,
    49→  containerWidth,
    50→  reducedSpacing,
    51→  columnsPerRow,
    52→  itemsPerPage,

    55→    () => calculateGalleryLayout(projectAspectRatio, isMobile, containerWidth, undefined, reducedSpacing),
    56→    [projectAspectRatio, isMobile, containerWidth, reducedSpacing],
    57→  );
    58→
    59→  const effectiveColumnsPerRow = columnsPerRow === 'auto' ? aspectRatioLayout.columns : columnsPerRow;
    60→  const defaultItemsPerPage = aspectRatioLayout.itemsPerPage;
    61→
    62→  const rawItemsPerPage = itemsPerPage ?? defaultItemsPerPage;
    63→  const actualItemsPerPage =

    68→      GRID_COLUMN_CLASSES[effectiveColumnsPerRow as keyof typeof GRID_COLUMN_CLASSES] ||

    70→    [effectiveColumnsPerRow, aspectRatioLayout.gridColumnClasses],
    71→  );
    72→
    73→  return {
    74→    aspectRatioLayout,
    75→    effectiveColumnsPerRow,
    76→    actualItemsPerPage,
    77→    gridColumnClasses,
    78→  };
    79→}
