1→import {
     2→  useReducer,
     3→  useRef,
     4→  useEffect,
     5→  useMemo,
     6→  type Dispatch,
     7→} from 'react';
     8→import type { GeneratedImageWithMetadata } from '../types';
     9→
    10→// Consolidated state interface
    11→interface MediaGalleryState {
    12→  // Lightbox state
    13→  activeLightboxMedia: GeneratedImageWithMetadata | null;
    14→  autoEnterEditMode: boolean;
    15→  selectedImageForDetails: GeneratedImageWithMetadata | null;
    16→  showTaskDetailsModal: boolean;
    17→  pendingLightboxTarget: 'first' | 'last' | null;
    18→  
    19→  // Optimistic state
    20→  optimisticUnpositionedIds: Set<string>;
    21→  optimisticPositionedIds: Set<string>;
    22→  optimisticDeletedIds: Set<string>;
    23→  
    24→  // Shot selection state
    25→  selectedShotIdLocal: string;
    26→  
    27→  // UI state
    28→  showTickForImageId: string | null;
    29→  showTickForSecondaryImageId: string | null;
    30→  addingToShotImageId: string | null;
    31→  addingToShotWithoutPositionImageId: string | null;
    32→  downloadingImageId: string | null;
    33→  isDownloadingStarred: boolean;
    34→  
    35→  // Mobile state
    36→  mobileActiveImageId: string | null;
    37→  mobilePopoverOpenImageId: string | null;
    38→  
    39→  // Backfill state
    40→  isBackfillLoading: boolean;
    41→  backfillSkeletonCount: number;
    42→}
    43→
    44→type SettableMediaGalleryFields = {
    45→  activeLightboxMedia: MediaGalleryState['activeLightboxMedia'];
    46→  autoEnterEditMode: MediaGalleryState['autoEnterEditMode'];
    47→  selectedImageForDetails: MediaGalleryState['selectedImageForDetails'];
    48→  showTaskDetailsModal: MediaGalleryState['showTaskDetailsModal'];
    49→  pendingLightboxTarget: MediaGalleryState['pendingLightboxTarget'];
    50→  selectedShotIdLocal: MediaGalleryState['selectedShotIdLocal'];
    51→  showTickForImageId: MediaGalleryState['showTickForImageId'];
    52→  showTickForSecondaryImageId: MediaGalleryState['showTickForSecondaryImageId'];
    53→  addingToShotImageId: MediaGalleryState['addingToShotImageId'];
    54→  addingToShotWithoutPositionImageId: MediaGalleryState['addingToShotWithoutPositionImageId'];
    55→  downloadingImageId: MediaGalleryState['downloadingImageId'];
    56→  isDownloadingStarred: MediaGalleryState['isDownloadingStarred'];
    57→  mobileActiveImageId: MediaGalleryState['mobileActiveImageId'];
    58→  mobilePopoverOpenImageId: MediaGalleryState['mobilePopoverOpenImageId'];
    59→  isBackfillLoading: MediaGalleryState['isBackfillLoading'];
    60→  backfillSkeletonCount: MediaGalleryState['backfillSkeletonCount'];
    61→};
    62→
    63→type SetFieldAction = {
    64→  [Field in keyof SettableMediaGalleryFields]: {
    65→    type: 'SET_FIELD';
    66→    field: Field;
    67→    payload: SettableMediaGalleryFields[Field];
    68→  };
    69→}[keyof SettableMediaGalleryFields];
    70→
    71→// Action types for the reducer
    72→type MediaGalleryStateAction =
    73→  | SetFieldAction
    74→  | { type: 'MARK_OPTIMISTIC_UNPOSITIONED'; payload: { mediaId: string; shotId: string } }
    75→  | { type: 'MARK_OPTIMISTIC_POSITIONED'; payload: { mediaId: string; shotId: string } }
    76→  | { type: 'MARK_OPTIMISTIC_DELETED'; payload: string }
    77→  | { type: 'MARK_OPTIMISTIC_DELETED_WITH_BACKFILL'; payload: string } // Combined action for atomic update
    78→  | { type: 'REMOVE_OPTIMISTIC_DELETED'; payload: string }
    79→  | { type: 'RECONCILE_OPTIMISTIC_STATE'; payload: Set<string> }
    80→  | { type: 'RESET_UI_STATE' };
    81→
    82→// Initial state factory
    83→const createInitialState = (
    84→  currentShotId?: string,
    85→  lastShotId?: string,
    86→  simplifiedShotOptions: { id: string; name: string }[] = []
    87→): MediaGalleryState => ({
    88→  // Lightbox state
    89→  activeLightboxMedia: null,
    90→  autoEnterEditMode: false,
    91→  selectedImageForDetails: null,
    92→  showTaskDetailsModal: false,
    93→  pendingLightboxTarget: null,
    94→  
    95→  // Optimistic state
    96→  optimisticUnpositionedIds: new Set(),
    97→  optimisticPositionedIds: new Set(),
    98→  optimisticDeletedIds: new Set(),
    99→  
   100→  // Shot selection state
   101→  selectedShotIdLocal: currentShotId || lastShotId || (simplifiedShotOptions.length > 0 ? simplifiedShotOptions[0].id : ""),
   102→  
   103→  // UI state
   104→  showTickForImageId: null,
   105→  showTickForSecondaryImageId: null,
   106→  addingToShotImageId: null,
   107→  addingToShotWithoutPositionImageId: null,
   108→  downloadingImageId: null,
   109→  isDownloadingStarred: false,
   110→  
   111→  // Mobile state
   112→  mobileActiveImageId: null,
   113→  mobilePopoverOpenImageId: null,
   114→  
   115→  // Backfill state
   116→  isBackfillLoading: false,
   117→  backfillSkeletonCount: 0,
   118→});
   119→
   120→// Optimized reducer with batched updates
   121→const mediaGalleryStateReducer = (
   122→  state: MediaGalleryState,
   123→  action: MediaGalleryStateAction
   124→): MediaGalleryState => {
   125→  switch (action.type) {
   126→    case 'SET_FIELD':
   127→      return {
   128→        ...state,
   129→        [action.field]: action.payload,
   130→      };
   131→      
   132→    case 'MARK_OPTIMISTIC_UNPOSITIONED': {
   133→      // Store composite key: mediaId:shotId
   134→      const { mediaId, shotId } = action.payload;
   135→      const key = `${mediaId}:${shotId}`;
   136→      const newUnpositioned = new Set(state.optimisticUnpositionedIds);
   137→      const newPositioned = new Set(state.optimisticPositionedIds);
   138→      newUnpositioned.add(key);
   139→      newPositioned.delete(key);
   140→      return {
   141→        ...state,
   142→        optimisticUnpositionedIds: newUnpositioned,
   143→        optimisticPositionedIds: newPositioned,
   144→      };
   145→    }
   146→    
   147→    case 'MARK_OPTIMISTIC_POSITIONED': {
   148→      // Store composite key: mediaId:shotId
   149→      const { mediaId, shotId } = action.payload;
   150→      const key = `${mediaId}:${shotId}`;
   151→      const newPositioned = new Set(state.optimisticPositionedIds);
   152→      const newUnpositioned = new Set(state.optimisticUnpositionedIds);
   153→      newPositioned.add(key);
   154→      newUnpositioned.delete(key);
   155→      return {
   156→        ...state,
   157→        optimisticPositionedIds: newPositioned,
   158→        optimisticUnpositionedIds: newUnpositioned,
   159→      };
   160→    }
   161→    
   162→    case 'MARK_OPTIMISTIC_DELETED': {
   163→      const newDeleted = new Set(state.optimisticDeletedIds);
   164→      newDeleted.add(action.payload);
   165→      return { ...state, optimisticDeletedIds: newDeleted };
   166→    }
   167→
   168→    case 'MARK_OPTIMISTIC_DELETED_WITH_BACKFILL': {
   169→      // Combined action: mark deleted AND enable backfill loading in ONE state update
   170→      // This ensures skeleton appears in the same render where item disappears
   171→      const newDeleted = new Set(state.optimisticDeletedIds);
   172→      newDeleted.add(action.payload);
   173→      return { ...state, optimisticDeletedIds: newDeleted, isBackfillLoading: true };
   174→    }
   175→
   176→    case 'REMOVE_OPTIMISTIC_DELETED': {
   177→      const newDeleted = new Set(state.optimisticDeletedIds);
   178→      newDeleted.delete(action.payload);
   179→      return { ...state, optimisticDeletedIds: newDeleted };
   180→    }
   181→    
   182→    case 'RECONCILE_OPTIMISTIC_STATE': {
   183→      const currentImageIds = action.payload;
   184→
   185→      // Clean up optimistic sets - remove entries for images no longer in the list
   186→      // Composite keys are in format mediaId:shotId
   187→      const newUnpositioned = new Set<string>();
   188→      for (const key of state.optimisticUnpositionedIds) {
   189→        const mediaId = key.split(':')[0];
   190→        if (currentImageIds.has(mediaId)) {
   191→          newUnpositioned.add(key);
   192→        }
   193→      }
   194→
   195→      const newPositioned = new Set<string>();
   196→      for (const key of state.optimisticPositionedIds) {
   197→        const mediaId = key.split(':')[0];
   198→        if (currentImageIds.has(mediaId)) {
   199→          newPositioned.add(key);
   200→        }
   201→      }
   202→
   203→      const newDeleted = new Set<string>();
   204→      for (const id of state.optimisticDeletedIds) {
   205→        if (currentImageIds.has(id)) {
   206→          newDeleted.add(id);
   207→        }
   208→      }
   209→      
   210→      return {
   211→        ...state,
   212→        optimisticUnpositionedIds: newUnpositioned,
   213→        optimisticPositionedIds: newPositioned,
   214→        optimisticDeletedIds: newDeleted,
   215→      };
   216→    }
   217→    
   218→    case 'RESET_UI_STATE':
   219→      return {
   220→        ...state,
   221→        showTickForImageId: null,
   222→        showTickForSecondaryImageId: null,
   223→        addingToShotImageId: null,
   224→        addingToShotWithoutPositionImageId: null,
   225→        downloadingImageId: null,
   226→        isDownloadingStarred: false,
   227→        mobileActiveImageId: null,
   228→        mobilePopoverOpenImageId: null,
   229→      };
   230→      
   231→    default:
   232→      return state;
   233→  }
   234→};
   235→
   236→const createFieldSetter = <Field extends keyof SettableMediaGalleryFields>(
   237→  dispatch: Dispatch<MediaGalleryStateAction>,
   238→  field: Field
   239→) => (value: SettableMediaGalleryFields[Field]) => {
   240→  dispatch({
   241→    type: 'SET_FIELD',
   242→    field,
   243→    payload: value,
   244→  });
   245→};
   246→
   247→interface UseMediaGalleryStateOptimizedProps {
   248→  images: GeneratedImageWithMetadata[];
   249→  currentShotId?: string;
   250→  lastShotId?: string;
   251→  simplifiedShotOptions: { id: string; name: string }[];
   252→  isServerPagination?: boolean;
   253→  serverPage?: number;
   254→}
   255→
   256→interface UseMediaGalleryStateOptimizedReturn {
   257→  // State
   258→  state: MediaGalleryState;
   259→  
   260→  // Actions
   261→  setActiveLightboxMedia: (media: GeneratedImageWithMetadata | null) => void;
   262→  setAutoEnterEditMode: (value: boolean) => void;
   263→  setSelectedImageForDetails: (image: GeneratedImageWithMetadata | null) => void;
   264→  setShowTaskDetailsModal: (show: boolean) => void;
   265→  setPendingLightboxTarget: (target: 'first' | 'last' | null) => void;
   266→  markOptimisticUnpositioned: (imageId: string, shotId: string) => void;
   267→  markOptimisticPositioned: (imageId: string, shotId: string) => void;
   268→  markOptimisticDeleted: (imageId: string) => void;
   269→  markOptimisticDeletedWithBackfill: (imageId: string) => void;
   270→  removeOptimisticDeleted: (imageId: string) => void;
   271→  setSelectedShotIdLocal: (id: string) => void;
   272→  setShowTickForImageId: (id: string | null) => void;
   273→  setShowTickForSecondaryImageId: (id: string | null) => void;
   274→  setAddingToShotImageId: (id: string | null) => void;
   275→  setAddingToShotWithoutPositionImageId: (id: string | null) => void;
   276→  setDownloadingImageId: (id: string | null) => void;
   277→  setIsDownloadingStarred: (downloading: boolean) => void;
   278→  setMobileActiveImageId: (id: string | null) => void;
   279→  setMobilePopoverOpenImageId: (id: string | null) => void;
   280→  setIsBackfillLoading: (loading: boolean) => void;
   281→  setBackfillSkeletonCount: (count: number) => void;
   282→  resetUIState: () => void;
   283→  
   284→  // Refs (unchanged)
   285→  mainTickTimeoutRef: React.MutableRefObject<NodeJS.Timeout | null>;
   286→  secondaryTickTimeoutRef: React.MutableRefObject<NodeJS.Timeout | null>;
   287→  galleryTopRef: React.MutableRefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>;
   288→  safetyTimeoutRef: React.MutableRefObject<NodeJS.Timeout | null>;
   289→}
   290→
   291→function clearTimer(timeoutRef: React.MutableRefObject<NodeJS.Timeout | null>): void {
   292→  const timeoutId = timeoutRef.current;
   293→  if (!timeoutId) {
   294→    return;
   295→  }
   296→
   297→  clearTimeout(timeoutId);
   298→  timeoutRef.current = null;
   299→}
   300→
   301→export const useMediaGalleryStateOptimized = ({
   302→  images,
   303→  currentShotId,
   304→  lastShotId,
   305→  simplifiedShotOptions,
   306→}: UseMediaGalleryStateOptimizedProps): UseMediaGalleryStateOptimizedReturn => {
   307→  
   308→  // Initialize state with useReducer instead of multiple useState calls
   309→  const [state, dispatch] = useReducer(
   310→    mediaGalleryStateReducer,
   311→    createInitialState(currentShotId, lastShotId, simplifiedShotOptions)
   312→  );
   313→  
   314→  // Memoized action creators to prevent unnecessary re-renders
   315→  const actions = useMemo(() => ({
   316→    setActiveLightboxMedia: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'activeLightboxMedia'),
   317→    setAutoEnterEditMode: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'autoEnterEditMode'),
   318→    setSelectedImageForDetails: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'selectedImageForDetails'),
   319→    setShowTaskDetailsModal: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'showTaskDetailsModal'),
   320→    setPendingLightboxTarget: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'pendingLightboxTarget'),
   321→    markOptimisticUnpositioned: (imageId: string, shotId: string) => 
   322→      dispatch({ type: 'MARK_OPTIMISTIC_UNPOSITIONED', payload: { mediaId: imageId, shotId } }),
   323→    markOptimisticPositioned: (imageId: string, shotId: string) => 
   324→      dispatch({ type: 'MARK_OPTIMISTIC_POSITIONED', payload: { mediaId: imageId, shotId } }),
   325→    markOptimisticDeleted: (imageId: string) =>
   326→      dispatch({ type: 'MARK_OPTIMISTIC_DELETED', payload: imageId }),
   327→    markOptimisticDeletedWithBackfill: (imageId: string) =>
   328→      dispatch({ type: 'MARK_OPTIMISTIC_DELETED_WITH_BACKFILL', payload: imageId }),
   329→    removeOptimisticDeleted: (imageId: string) => 
   330→      dispatch({ type: 'REMOVE_OPTIMISTIC_DELETED', payload: imageId }),
   331→    setSelectedShotIdLocal: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'selectedShotIdLocal'),
   332→    setShowTickForImageId: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'showTickForImageId'),
   333→    setShowTickForSecondaryImageId: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'showTickForSecondaryImageId'),
   334→    setAddingToShotImageId: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'addingToShotImageId'),
   335→    setAddingToShotWithoutPositionImageId: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'addingToShotWithoutPositionImageId'),
   336→    setDownloadingImageId: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'downloadingImageId'),
   337→    setIsDownloadingStarred: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'isDownloadingStarred'),
   338→    setMobileActiveImageId: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'mobileActiveImageId'),
   339→    setMobilePopoverOpenImageId: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'mobilePopoverOpenImageId'),
   340→    setIsBackfillLoading: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'isBackfillLoading'),
   341→    setBackfillSkeletonCount: createFieldSetter(dispatch, 'backfillSkeletonCount'),
   342→    resetUIState: () =>
   343→      dispatch({ type: 'RESET_UI_STATE' }),
   344→  }), []);
   345→  
   346→  // Refs (unchanged from original)
   347→  const mainTickTimeoutRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);
   348→  const secondaryTickTimeoutRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);
   349→  const galleryTopRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
   350→  const safetyTimeoutRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);
   351→
   352→  // Track previous lastShotId to detect when user navigates to a different shot
   353→  const prevLastShotIdRef = useRef<string | undefined>(lastShotId);
   354→  
   355→  // Sync selectedShotIdLocal when:
   356→  // 1. lastShotId changes (user navigated to a different shot)
   357→  // 2. Current selection is invalid (empty or shot no longer exists)
   358→  useEffect(() => {
   359→    const isCurrentSelectionValid = state.selectedShotIdLocal && simplifiedShotOptions.find(shot => shot.id === state.selectedShotIdLocal);
   360→    const lastShotIdChanged = lastShotId && lastShotId !== prevLastShotIdRef.current;
   361→    
   362→    // Update ref for next comparison
   363→    prevLastShotIdRef.current = lastShotId;
   364→    
   365→    // Sync when lastShotId changes (user clicked into a shot)
   366→    if (lastShotIdChanged && lastShotId !== state.selectedShotIdLocal) {
   367→      actions.setSelectedShotIdLocal(lastShotId);
   368→      return;
   369→    }
   370→    
   371→    // Fix invalid selections (empty or shot no longer exists)
   372→    if (!isCurrentSelectionValid) {
   373→      const newSelection = lastShotId || (simplifiedShotOptions.length > 0 ? simplifiedShotOptions[0].id : "");
   374→      if (newSelection && newSelection !== state.selectedShotIdLocal) {
   375→        actions.setSelectedShotIdLocal(newSelection);
   376→      }
   377→    }
   378→  }, [lastShotId, simplifiedShotOptions, state.selectedShotIdLocal, actions]);
   379→
   380→  // Memoize image IDs to prevent unnecessary effect triggers
   381→  const currentImageIds = useMemo(() => 
   382→    new Set(images.map(img => img.id)), 
   383→    [images]
   384→  );
   385→
   386→  // Reconcile optimistic state when images update
   387→  useEffect(() => {
   388→    // Sync activeLightboxMedia with updated images list to ensure fresh data (like name changes)
   389→    if (state.activeLightboxMedia) {
   390→      const activeLightbox = state.activeLightboxMedia;
   391→      const updatedImage = images.find(img => img.id === activeLightbox.id);
   392→      // Only update if the object reference changed (meaning data changed or refetched)
   393→      // and deep equality check on key properties to avoid unnecessary render cycles
   394→      if (updatedImage && updatedImage !== state.activeLightboxMedia) {
   395→        // Check if relevant fields actually changed to prevent loop.
   396→        const nameChanged = updatedImage.name !== state.activeLightboxMedia.name;
   397→        const starredChanged = updatedImage.starred !== state.activeLightboxMedia.starred;
   398→        const urlChanged = updatedImage.url !== state.activeLightboxMedia.url;
   399→
   400→        if (nameChanged || starredChanged || urlChanged) {
   401→          const autoEnterFlag = state.activeLightboxMedia.metadata?.__autoEnterEditMode as boolean | undefined;
   402→          actions.setActiveLightboxMedia({
   403→            ...updatedImage,
   404→            metadata: autoEnterFlag === undefined
   405→              ? updatedImage.metadata
   406→              : { ...updatedImage.metadata, __autoEnterEditMode: autoEnterFlag },
   407→          });
   408→        }
   409→      }
   410→    }
   411→
   412→    // Clean up optimistic sets using the consolidated action
   413→    dispatch({ type: 'RECONCILE_OPTIMISTIC_STATE', payload: currentImageIds });
   414→  }, [currentImageIds, images, actions, state.activeLightboxMedia]);
   415→
   416→  // Cleanup timeouts on unmount
   417→  useEffect(() => {
   418→    return () => {
   419→      clearTimer(mainTickTimeoutRef);
   420→      clearTimer(secondaryTickTimeoutRef);
   421→      clearTimer(safetyTimeoutRef);
   422→    };
   423→  }, []);
   424→
   425→  return {
   426→    state,
   427→    ...actions,
   428→    
   429→    // Refs
   430→    mainTickTimeoutRef,
   431→    secondaryTickTimeoutRef,
   432→    galleryTopRef,
   433→    safetyTimeoutRef,
   434→  };
   435→};
   436→

1→import { useReducer, useCallback, useMemo, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
     2→import type { GeneratedImageWithMetadata } from '../types';
     3→import { GalleryFilterState, DEFAULT_GALLERY_FILTERS } from '../types';
     4→import { hasVideoExtension } from '@/shared/lib/typeGuards';
     5→
     6→// Maps GalleryFilterState keys to reducer action types
     7→const FILTER_ACTIONS = {
     8→  mediaType: 'SET_MEDIA_TYPE',
     9→  shotFilter: 'SET_SHOT_FILTER',
    10→  excludePositioned: 'SET_EXCLUDE_POSITIONED',
    11→  searchTerm: 'SET_SEARCH_TERM',
    12→  starredOnly: 'SET_STARRED_ONLY',
    13→  toolTypeFilter: 'SET_TOOL_TYPE_FILTER',
    14→} as const;
    15→
    16→interface InternalFiltersState {
    17→  mediaType: 'all' | 'image' | 'video';
    18→  shotFilter: string;
    19→  excludePositioned: boolean;
    20→  starredOnly: boolean;
    21→  toolTypeFilter: boolean;
    22→  searchTerm: string;
    23→  isSearchOpen: boolean;
    24→}
    25→
    26→type InternalFiltersAction =
    27→  | { type: 'SET_MEDIA_TYPE'; payload: 'all' | 'image' | 'video' }
    28→  | { type: 'SET_SHOT_FILTER'; payload: string }
    29→  | { type: 'SET_EXCLUDE_POSITIONED'; payload: boolean }
    30→  | { type: 'SET_STARRED_ONLY'; payload: boolean }
    31→  | { type: 'SET_TOOL_TYPE_FILTER'; payload: boolean }
    32→  | { type: 'SET_SEARCH_TERM'; payload: string }
    33→  | { type: 'SET_IS_SEARCH_OPEN'; payload: boolean }
    34→  | { type: 'CLEAR_SEARCH' };
    35→
    36→const internalReducer = (
    37→  state: InternalFiltersState,
    38→  action: InternalFiltersAction
    39→): InternalFiltersState => {
    40→  switch (action.type) {
    41→    case 'SET_MEDIA_TYPE':
    42→      return { ...state, mediaType: action.payload };
    43→    case 'SET_SHOT_FILTER':
    44→      return { ...state, shotFilter: action.payload };
    45→    case 'SET_EXCLUDE_POSITIONED':
    46→      return { ...state, excludePositioned: action.payload };
    47→    case 'SET_STARRED_ONLY':
    48→      return { ...state, starredOnly: action.payload };
    49→    case 'SET_TOOL_TYPE_FILTER':
    50→      return { ...state, toolTypeFilter: action.payload };
    51→    case 'SET_SEARCH_TERM':
    52→      return {
    53→        ...state,
    54→        searchTerm: action.payload,
    55→        isSearchOpen: state.isSearchOpen || !!action.payload,
    56→      };
    57→    case 'SET_IS_SEARCH_OPEN':
    58→      return { ...state, isSearchOpen: action.payload };
    59→    case 'CLEAR_SEARCH':
    60→      return { ...state, searchTerm: '', isSearchOpen: false };
    61→    default:
    62→      return state;
    63→  }
    64→};
    65→
    66→interface UseMediaGalleryFiltersOptimizedProps {
    67→  images: GeneratedImageWithMetadata[];
    68→  optimisticDeletedIds: Set<string>;
    69→  currentToolType?: string;
    70→  initialFilterState?: boolean;
    71→  onServerPageChange?: (page: number, fromBottom?: boolean) => void;
    72→  serverPage?: number;
    73→  filters?: GalleryFilterState;
    74→  onFiltersChange?: (filters: GalleryFilterState) => void;
    75→  defaultFilters?: Partial<GalleryFilterState>;
    76→}
    77→
    78→export const useMediaGalleryFiltersOptimized = ({
    79→  images,
    80→  optimisticDeletedIds,
    81→  currentToolType,
    82→  initialFilterState = true,
    83→  onServerPageChange,
    84→  serverPage,
    85→  filters: controlledFilters,
    86→  onFiltersChange,
    87→  defaultFilters,
    88→}: UseMediaGalleryFiltersOptimizedProps) => {
    89→  const isControlled = controlledFilters !== undefined;
    90→
    91→   
    92→  const mergedDefaults = useMemo((): GalleryFilterState => ({
    93→    ...DEFAULT_GALLERY_FILTERS,
    94→    ...defaultFilters,
    95→  }), [defaultFilters]);
    96→
    97→  // Reducer owns filter values (uncontrolled) and isSearchOpen (both modes)
    98→  const [internalState, dispatch] = useReducer(internalReducer, {
    99→    mediaType: mergedDefaults.mediaType,
   100→    shotFilter: mergedDefaults.shotFilter,
   101→    excludePositioned: mergedDefaults.excludePositioned,
   102→    starredOnly: mergedDefaults.starredOnly,
   103→    toolTypeFilter: mergedDefaults.toolTypeFilter,
   104→    searchTerm: mergedDefaults.searchTerm,
   105→    isSearchOpen: !!mergedDefaults.searchTerm,
   106→  });
   107→
   108→  const searchInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
   109→
   110→  // Filter values: controlled reads from props, uncontrolled reads from reducer
   111→  const currentFilters: GalleryFilterState = isControlled
   112→    ? controlledFilters
   113→    : {
   114→        mediaType: internalState.mediaType,
   115→        shotFilter: internalState.shotFilter,
   116→        excludePositioned: internalState.excludePositioned,
   117→        searchTerm: internalState.searchTerm,
   118→        starredOnly: internalState.starredOnly,
   119→        toolTypeFilter: internalState.toolTypeFilter,
   120→      };
   121→
   122→  // isSearchOpen is always internal — it's UI chrome, not filter state
   123→  const isSearchOpen = internalState.isSearchOpen;
   124→
   125→  // Controlled mode calls onFiltersChange, uncontrolled dispatches to reducer
   126→  const updateFilter = useCallback(<K extends keyof GalleryFilterState>(
   127→    key: K, value: GalleryFilterState[K]
   128→  ) => {
   129→    if (isControlled) {
   130→      onFiltersChange?.({ ...controlledFilters!, [key]: value });
   131→    } else {
   132→      dispatch({ type: FILTER_ACTIONS[key], payload: value } as InternalFiltersAction);
   133→    }
   134→  }, [isControlled, controlledFilters, onFiltersChange]);
   135→
   136→  const setMediaTypeFilter = useCallback((v: 'all' | 'image' | 'video') => updateFilter('mediaType', v), [updateFilter]);
   137→  const setShotFilter = useCallback((v: string) => updateFilter('shotFilter', v), [updateFilter]);
   138→  const setExcludePositioned = useCallback((v: boolean) => updateFilter('excludePositioned', v), [updateFilter]);
   139→  const setShowStarredOnly = useCallback((v: boolean) => updateFilter('starredOnly', v), [updateFilter]);
   140→  const setToolTypeFilterEnabled = useCallback((v: boolean) => updateFilter('toolTypeFilter', v), [updateFilter]);
   141→
   142→  const setSearchTerm = useCallback((v: string) => {
   143→    updateFilter('searchTerm', v);
   144→    // Auto-open search UI when setting a non-empty term in controlled mode
   145→    // (uncontrolled mode handles this in the SET_SEARCH_TERM reducer case)
   146→    if (isControlled && v) dispatch({ type: 'SET_IS_SEARCH_OPEN', payload: true });
   147→  }, [updateFilter, isControlled]);
   148→
   149→  const setIsSearchOpen = useCallback((v: boolean) => {
   150→    dispatch({ type: 'SET_IS_SEARCH_OPEN', payload: v });
   151→  }, []);
   152→
   153→  const toggleSearch = useCallback(() => {
   154→    const opening = !internalState.isSearchOpen;
   155→    dispatch({ type: 'SET_IS_SEARCH_OPEN', payload: opening });
   156→  }, [internalState.isSearchOpen]);
   157→
   158→  useEffect(() => {
   159→    if (isSearchOpen) {
   160→      searchInputRef.current?.focus();
   161→    }
   162→  }, [isSearchOpen]);
   163→
   164→  const clearSearch = useCallback(() => {
   165→    if (isControlled) {
   166→      onFiltersChange?.({ ...controlledFilters!, searchTerm: '' });
   167→    }
   168→    dispatch({ type: 'CLEAR_SEARCH' });
   169→  }, [isControlled, controlledFilters, onFiltersChange]);
   170→
   171→  const filteredImages = useMemo(() => {
   172→    let currentFiltered = images;
   173→
   174→    currentFiltered = currentFiltered.filter(image => !optimisticDeletedIds.has(image.id));
   175→
   176→    // Tool type filter (client pagination only)
   177→    const isServerPagination = !!(onServerPageChange && serverPage);
   178→    if (!isServerPagination && initialFilterState && currentFilters.toolTypeFilter && currentToolType) {
   179→      currentFiltered = currentFiltered.filter(image => {
   180→        const metadata = image.metadata;
   181→        if (!metadata || !metadata.tool_type) return false;
   182→        if (metadata.tool_type === currentToolType) return true;
   183→        if (metadata.tool_type === `${currentToolType}-reconstructed-client`) return true;
   184→        return false;
   185→      });
   186→    }
   187→
   188→    if (!isServerPagination && currentFilters.mediaType !== 'all') {
   189→      currentFiltered = currentFiltered.filter(image => {
   190→        const isActuallyVideo = typeof image.isVideo === 'boolean' ? image.isVideo : hasVideoExtension(image.url);
   191→        if (currentFilters.mediaType === 'image') return !isActuallyVideo;
   192→        if (currentFilters.mediaType === 'video') return isActuallyVideo;
   193→        return true;
   194→      });
   195→    }
   196→
   197→    if (!isServerPagination && currentFilters.starredOnly) {
   198→      currentFiltered = currentFiltered.filter(image => image.starred === true);
   199→    }
   200→
   201→    if (!isServerPagination && currentFilters.searchTerm.trim()) {
   202→      currentFiltered = currentFiltered.filter(image => {
   203→        const prompt = image.prompt ||
   204→                      image.metadata?.prompt ||
   205→                      image.metadata?.originalParams?.orchestrator_details?.prompt ||
   206→                      '';
   207→        return prompt.toLowerCase().includes(currentFilters.searchTerm.toLowerCase());
   208→      });
   209→    }
   210→
   211→    return currentFiltered;
   212→  }, [
   213→    images,
   214→    optimisticDeletedIds,
   215→    initialFilterState,
   216→    currentFilters.toolTypeFilter,
   217→    currentFilters.mediaType,
   218→    currentFilters.starredOnly,
   219→    currentFilters.searchTerm,
   220→    currentToolType,
   221→    onServerPageChange,
   222→    serverPage,
   223→  ]);
   224→
   225→  return {
   226→    filterByToolType: initialFilterState,
   227→    mediaTypeFilter: currentFilters.mediaType,
   228→    shotFilter: currentFilters.shotFilter,
   229→    excludePositioned: currentFilters.excludePositioned,
   230→    showStarredOnly: currentFilters.starredOnly,
   231→    toolTypeFilterEnabled: currentFilters.toolTypeFilter,
   232→    searchTerm: currentFilters.searchTerm,
   233→    isSearchOpen,
   234→    searchInputRef,
   235→
   236→    setMediaTypeFilter,
   237→    setShotFilter,
   238→    setExcludePositioned,
   239→    setShowStarredOnly,
   240→    setToolTypeFilterEnabled,
   241→    setSearchTerm,
   242→    setIsSearchOpen,
   243→
   244→    filteredImages,
   245→    toggleSearch,
   246→    clearSearch,
   247→  };
   248→};
   249→

1→/**
     2→ * Derive input images from task params
     3→ * Strips any surrounding quotes from URLs that may have been improperly stored
     4→ */
     5→export const deriveGalleryInputImages = (task: { params?: unknown } | null | undefined): string[] => {
     6→  const cleanUrl = (url: string): string => {
     7→    if (typeof url !== 'string') return url;
     8→    // Remove surrounding quotes if present
     9→    return url.replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '');
    10→  };
    11→
    12→  const params = (
    13→    task && typeof task === 'object' && 'params' in task
    14→      ? (task as { params?: unknown }).params
    15→      : {}
    16→  ) as Record<string, unknown>;
    17→  if (Array.isArray(params.input_images) && params.input_images.length > 0) {
    18→    return (params.input_images as string[]).map(cleanUrl);
    19→  }
    20→  const payload = params.full_orchestrator_payload as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
    21→  if (payload && Array.isArray(payload.input_image_paths_resolved)) {
    22→    return (payload.input_image_paths_resolved as string[]).map(cleanUrl);
    23→  }
    24→  if (Array.isArray(params.input_image_paths_resolved)) {
    25→    return (params.input_image_paths_resolved as string[]).map(cleanUrl);
    26→  }
    27→  return [];
    28→};
    29→

1→interface ScrollToGalleryTopOptions {
     2→  galleryTop: HTMLElement;
     3→  isMobile: boolean;
     4→}
     5→
     6→/**
     7→ * Keep DOM-specific scroll behavior out of pagination state hooks.
     8→ */
     9→export function scrollToGalleryTop(options: ScrollToGalleryTopOptions): void {
    10→  const rect = options.galleryTop.getBoundingClientRect();
    11→  const scrollTop = window.pageYOffset || document.documentElement.scrollTop;
    12→  const targetPosition = rect.top + scrollTop - (options.isMobile ? 80 : 20);
    13→
    14→  window.scrollTo({
    15→    top: Math.max(0, targetPosition),
    16→    behavior: 'smooth',
    17→  });
    18→}
    19→
```

1→import {
     2→  DEFAULT_ROWS_PER_PAGE,
     3→  GRID_COLUMN_CLASSES,
     4→  ROW_LIMITS,
     5→  SKELETON_COLUMNS,
     6→  TARGET_IMAGE_WIDTH,
     7→} from './mediaGallery-constants';
     8→
     9→function parseAspectRatio(aspectRatioStr: string | undefined | null): number {
    10→  if (!aspectRatioStr) {
    11→    return 16 / 9;
    12→  }
    13→
    14→  const parts = aspectRatioStr.split(':');
    15→  if (parts.length !== 2) {
    16→    return 16 / 9;
    17→  }
    18→
    19→  const width = parseFloat(parts[0]);
    20→  const height = parseFloat(parts[1]);
    21→  if (Number.isNaN(width) || Number.isNaN(height) || height === 0) {
    22→    return 16 / 9;
    23→  }
    24→
    25→  return width / height;
    26→}
    27→
    28→function getEffectiveGap(reducedSpacing: boolean): number {
    29→  if (!reducedSpacing) {
    30→    return 16;
    31→  }
    32→
    33→  const isSmOrAbove = typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.innerWidth >= 640;
    34→  return isSmOrAbove ? 16 : 8;
    35→}
    36→
    37→function getColumnsForAspectRatio(aspectRatio: number): number {
    38→  const baseColumns = 5;
    39→  const computed = Math.round(baseColumns / Math.sqrt(aspectRatio)) + 2;
    40→  return Math.max(5, Math.min(9, computed));
    41→}
    42→
    43→function getTargetImageWidth(): number {
    44→  return TARGET_IMAGE_WIDTH.BASE;
    45→}
    46→
    47→function calculateDynamicColumns(
    48→  containerWidth: number,
    49→  aspectRatio: number,
    50→  gap: number = 16
    51→): number {
    52→  if (!containerWidth || containerWidth <= 0) {
    53→    return getColumnsForAspectRatio(aspectRatio);
    54→  }
    55→
    56→  const targetWidth = getTargetImageWidth();
    57→  const effectiveWidth = targetWidth + gap;
    58→  const columns = Math.floor((containerWidth + gap) / effectiveWidth);
    59→  return Math.max(
    60→    TARGET_IMAGE_WIDTH.MIN_COLUMNS,
    61→    Math.min(TARGET_IMAGE_WIDTH.MAX_COLUMNS, columns)
    62→  );
    63→}
    64→
    65→function calculateDynamicRows(
    66→  containerWidth: number,
    67→  containerHeight: number,
    68→  columns: number,
    69→  aspectRatio: number,
    70→  gap: number = 16
    71→): number {
    72→  if (!containerWidth || containerWidth <= 0 || !containerHeight || containerHeight <= 0) {
    73→    return DEFAULT_ROWS_PER_PAGE.DESKTOP;
    74→  }
    75→
    76→  const totalGapWidth = (columns - 1) * gap;
    77→  const imageWidth = (containerWidth - totalGapWidth) / columns;
    78→  const imageHeight = imageWidth / aspectRatio;
    79→  const effectiveHeight = imageHeight + gap;
    80→  const usableHeight = containerHeight - imageHeight * 0.5;
    81→  const rows = Math.floor((usableHeight + gap) / effectiveHeight);
    82→
    83→  return Math.max(
    84→    ROW_LIMITS.MIN_ROWS,
    85→    Math.min(ROW_LIMITS.MAX_ROWS, rows)
    86→  );
    87→}
    88→
    89→export function calculateGalleryLayout(
    90→  aspectRatioStr: string | undefined | null,
    91→  isMobile: boolean,
    92→  containerWidth?: number,
    93→  containerHeight?: number,
    94→  reducedSpacing: boolean = false
    95→): {
    96→  columns: number;
    97→  rows: number;
    98→  itemsPerPage: number;
    99→  skeletonColumns: typeof SKELETON_COLUMNS[keyof typeof SKELETON_COLUMNS];
   100→  gridColumnClasses: string;
   101→} {
   102→  const ratio = parseAspectRatio(aspectRatioStr);
   103→  const gap = getEffectiveGap(reducedSpacing);
   104→
   105→  const rawColumns = containerWidth && containerWidth > 0
   106→    ? calculateDynamicColumns(containerWidth, ratio, gap)
   107→    : getColumnsForAspectRatio(ratio);
   108→
   109→  const clampedColumns = Math.max(3, Math.min(12, rawColumns)) as keyof typeof GRID_COLUMN_CLASSES;
   110→
   111→  const rows = (!isMobile && containerWidth && containerWidth > 0 && containerHeight && containerHeight > 0)
   112→    ? calculateDynamicRows(containerWidth, containerHeight, rawColumns, ratio, gap)
   113→    : (isMobile ? DEFAULT_ROWS_PER_PAGE.MOBILE : DEFAULT_ROWS_PER_PAGE.DESKTOP);
   114→
   115→  const itemsPerPage = rawColumns * rows;
   116→  const skeletonColumns = SKELETON_COLUMNS[clampedColumns] || SKELETON_COLUMNS[5];
   117→  const gridColumnClasses = GRID_COLUMN_CLASSES[clampedColumns];
   118→
   119→  return {
   120→    columns: rawColumns,
   121→    rows,
   122→    itemsPerPage,
   123→    skeletonColumns,
   124→    gridColumnClasses,
   125→  };
   126→}
   127→
```

    57→
    58→export const useMediaGalleryActions = ({
    59→  onDelete,
    60→  activeLightboxMedia,
    61→  setActiveLightboxMedia,
    62→  setAutoEnterEditMode,
    63→  markOptimisticDeleted,

    81→}: UseMediaGalleryActionsProps): UseMediaGalleryActionsReturn => {
    82→
    83→  const { setLastAffectedShotId } = useLastAffectedShot();
    84→
    85→  // Track in-flight delete operations for debouncing rapid deletions
    86→  const pendingDeletesRef = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
    87→  const refetchTimeoutRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);
    88→  const clearLoadingTimeoutRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);
    89→
    90→  /**
    91→   * Optimistic delete with skeleton placeholder at end of grid.
    92→   *
    93→   * Flow:
    94→   * 1. Mark item as deleted (item disappears, others shift)
    95→   * 2. Show skeleton at end of grid while waiting for backfill
    96→   * 3. Call server delete
    97→   * 4. On success: trigger immediate refetch (debounced 100ms for rapid deletes)
    98→   * 5. On failure: revert optimistic state, hide skeleton, show error
    99→   * 6. Check page bounds after refetch
   100→   */
   101→  const handleOptimisticDelete = useCallback(async (imageId: string) => {
   102→    // Use combined action for atomic state update - both deleted mark AND backfill loading
   103→    // in a single dispatch, ensuring skeleton appears in same render as item disappears
   104→    if (isServerPagination) {
   105→      markOptimisticDeletedWithBackfill(imageId);
   106→    } else {
   107→      markOptimisticDeleted(imageId);
   108→    }
   109→    pendingDeletesRef.current.add(imageId);
   110→
   111→    // 3. Close lightbox if this image is open
   112→    if (activeLightboxMedia?.id === imageId) {
   113→      setActiveLightboxMedia(null);
   114→    }
   115→
   116→    try {
   117→      // 4. Delete on server
   118→      if (onDelete) {
   119→        await onDelete(imageId);
   120→      }
   121→
   122→      // 5. Trigger refetch (debounced 100ms to batch rapid deletions)
   123→      if (isServerPagination && onBackfillRequest) {
   124→        // Clear existing timeouts (debounce and any pending clear)
   125→        if (refetchTimeoutRef.current) {
   126→          clearTimeout(refetchTimeoutRef.current);
   127→        }
   128→        if (clearLoadingTimeoutRef.current) {
   129→          clearTimeout(clearLoadingTimeoutRef.current);
   130→        }
   131→
   132→        // Short debounce to batch rapid deletions
   133→        refetchTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(async () => {
   134→
   135→          try {
   136→            await onBackfillRequest();
   137→
   138→            // Check page bounds after refetch
   139→            const newLastPage = computeBackfillLastPage(
   140→              totalCount,
   141→              pendingDeletesRef.current.size,
   142→              itemsPerPage,
   143→            );
   144→
   145→            if (serverPage && serverPage > newLastPage) {
   146→              onPageBoundsExceeded?.(newLastPage);
   147→            }
   148→
   149→            // Clear backfill loading after a short delay to allow image to load
   150→            // The skeleton stays visible during this time, then fades out
   151→            // Use ref so this can be cancelled if more deletes happen
   152→            clearLoadingTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
   153→              setIsBackfillLoading(false);
   154→              clearLoadingTimeoutRef.current = null;
   155→            }, INTERACTION_TIMING.galleryBackfillLoadingClearMs);
   156→          } catch (error) {
   157→            normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'useMediaGalleryActions', showToast: false });
   158→            setIsBackfillLoading(false);
   159→          }
   160→          // Clear pending deletes after refetch attempt
   161→          pendingDeletesRef.current.clear();
   162→        }, INTERACTION_TIMING.galleryDeleteRefetchDebounceMs);
   163→      }
   164→
   165→    } catch (error) {
   166→      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'useMediaGalleryActions', toastTitle: 'Delete Failed' });
   167→
   168→      // Revert optimistic state
   169→      removeOptimisticDeleted(imageId);
   170→      pendingDeletesRef.current.delete(imageId);
   171→
   172→      // Hide skeleton if no more pending deletes
   173→      if (pendingDeletesRef.current.size === 0) {
   174→        setIsBackfillLoading(false);
   175→      }
   176→    }
   177→  }, [
   178→    markOptimisticDeleted,
   179→    markOptimisticDeletedWithBackfill,
   180→    removeOptimisticDeleted,
   181→    onDelete,
   182→    activeLightboxMedia,
   183→    setActiveLightboxMedia,
   184→    isServerPagination,
   185→    serverPage,
   186→    onBackfillRequest,
   187→    itemsPerPage,
   188→    setIsBackfillLoading,
   189→    totalCount,
   190→    onPageBoundsExceeded
   191→  ]);
   192→

   204→        based_on: image.based_on,
   205→      },
   206→    });
   207→    setAutoEnterEditMode(autoEnterEditMode);

   209→

   214→
   215→  const handleDownloadImage = useCallback(async (rawUrl: string, filename: string, imageId?: string, isVideo?: boolean, originalContentType?: string) => {
   216→    const currentDownloadId = imageId || filename;
   217→    setDownloadingImageId(currentDownloadId);

   243→  }, [setDownloadingImageId]);
   244→

   246→    setShowTickForImageId(imageId);

1→import { useState, useEffect, useRef, RefObject } from 'react';
     2→
     3→/**
     4→ * Hook to measure a container's width using ResizeObserver.
     5→ * Returns the current width and a ref to attach to the container.
     6→ *
     7→ * Uses window.innerWidth as initial estimate to avoid layout shift on first render.
     8→ * Only updates width when layout is stable (avoids intermediate states during mount).
     9→ *
    10→ * @returns [ref, width] - Attach ref to container, width updates on resize
    11→ */
    12→export function useContainerWidth(): [RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>, number] {
    13→  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
    14→  // Start with window width as estimate to avoid 0 on first render
    15→  const initialEstimate = typeof window !== 'undefined'
    16→    ? Math.floor(window.innerWidth * 0.9)
    17→    : 800;
    18→  const [width, setWidth] = useState(initialEstimate);
    19→  const hasReceivedStableWidth = useRef(false);
    20→
    21→  useEffect(() => {
    22→    const element = containerRef.current;
    23→    if (!element) return;
    24→
    25→    let debounceTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
    26→
    27→    // Set up ResizeObserver - this is the source of truth for width
    28→    // Debounced to avoid rapid recalculations during animated resizes (e.g. pane lock/unlock)
    29→    const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver((entries) => {
    30→      for (const entry of entries) {
    31→        const newWidth = entry.contentRect.width;
    32→        if (newWidth <= 0) continue;
    33→
    34→        if (!hasReceivedStableWidth.current) {
    35→          // First measurement: apply immediately to prevent layout flash
    36→          // (e.g. gallery in a narrow panel starts with full-viewport estimate)
    37→          hasReceivedStableWidth.current = true;
    38→          setWidth(newWidth);
    39→        } else {
    40→          // Subsequent measurements: debounce for smooth animated resizes
    41→          if (debounceTimer) clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
    42→          debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => setWidth(newWidth), 150);
    43→        }
    44→      }
    45→    });
    46→
    47→    resizeObserver.observe(element);
    48→
    49→    // Fallback: if ResizeObserver hasn't fired after a short delay, use offsetWidth
    50→    const fallbackTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
    51→      if (!hasReceivedStableWidth.current && element.offsetWidth > 0) {
    52→        setWidth(element.offsetWidth);
    53→      }
    54→    }, 100);
    55→
    56→    return () => {
    57→      resizeObserver.disconnect();
    58→      clearTimeout(fallbackTimeout);
    59→      if (debounceTimer) clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
    60→    };
    61→  }, [initialEstimate]);
    62→
    63→  return [containerRef, width];
    64→}
    65→
    66→interface ContainerDimensions {
    67→  width: number;
    68→  height: number;
    69→}
    70→
    71→/**
    72→ * Hook to measure a container's width and the full viewport height.
    73→ * Returns dimensions and a ref to attach to the container.
    74→ *
    75→ * Width: measured from the container element
    76→ * Height: full viewport height (window.innerHeight) minus an offset for controls
    77→ *
    78→ * This is used for calculating how many gallery rows fit in a full viewport,
    79→ * regardless of scroll position or where the gallery is on the page.
    80→ *
    81→ * @param heightOffset - Pixels to subtract from viewport height (e.g., for header + pagination)
    82→ * @param skipHeaderDetection - Skip auto-detecting sticky/fixed headers (e.g., on mobile where there's no header)
    83→ * @returns [ref, dimensions] - Attach ref to container, dimensions update on resize
    84→ */
    85→export function useContainerDimensions(
    86→  heightOffset: number = 0,
    87→  skipHeaderDetection: boolean = false
    88→): [RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>, ContainerDimensions] {
    89→  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
    90→
    91→  // Start with window dimensions as estimate
    92→  const initialWidthEstimate = typeof window !== 'undefined'
    93→    ? Math.floor(window.innerWidth * 0.9)
    94→    : 800;
    95→  const initialHeightEstimate = typeof window !== 'undefined'
    96→    ? window.innerHeight - heightOffset
    97→    : 600;
    98→
    99→  const [dimensions, setDimensions] = useState<ContainerDimensions>({
   100→    width: initialWidthEstimate,
   101→    height: initialHeightEstimate,
   102→  });
   103→  const hasReceivedStableDimensions = useRef(false);
   104→
   105→  useEffect(() => {
   106→    const element = containerRef.current;
   107→    if (!element) return;
   108→
   109→    const updateDimensions = () => {
   110→      const newWidth = element.getBoundingClientRect().width;
   111→
   112→      // Detect sticky/fixed header height by scanning for header-like elements
   113→      // pinned at the top of the viewport (the site header is a sibling, not ancestor)
   114→      // Skip on mobile/iPad where there's no fixed header
   115→      let fixedChromeHeight = 0;
   116→      if (!skipHeaderDetection) {
   117→        const candidates = document.querySelectorAll('[class*="sticky"], [class*="fixed"]');
   118→        for (const el of candidates) {
   119→          const style = getComputedStyle(el);
   120→          if (style.position !== 'sticky' && style.position !== 'fixed') continue;
   121→          const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
   122→          // Must be at the top (within 10px) and header-sized (under 200px)
   123→          if (rect.top <= 10 && rect.height > 0 && rect.height < 200) {
   124→            fixedChromeHeight = Math.max(fixedChromeHeight, rect.height);
   125→          }
   126→        }
   127→      }
   128→
   129→      // Available height = viewport - fixed header - caller's offset (pagination, gallery chrome, etc.)
   130→      const availableHeight = window.innerHeight - fixedChromeHeight - heightOffset;
   131→
   132→      // Only accept dimensions that are reasonable
   133→      if (newWidth > initialWidthEstimate * 0.5 && availableHeight > 200) {
   134→        hasReceivedStableDimensions.current = true;
   135→        setDimensions({
   136→          width: newWidth,
   137→          height: Math.max(200, availableHeight),
   138→        });
   139→      }
   140→    };
   141→
   142→    let debounceTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
   143→    const debouncedUpdate = () => {
   144→      if (debounceTimer) clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
   145→      debounceTimer = setTimeout(updateDimensions, 150);
   146→    };
   147→
   148→    // Set up ResizeObserver for container width changes
   149→    // Debounced to avoid rapid recalculations during animated resizes (e.g. pane lock/unlock)
   150→    const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(() => {
   151→      debouncedUpdate();
   152→    });
   153→
   154→    resizeObserver.observe(element);
   155→
   156→    // Also listen to window resize for viewport height changes
   157→    window.addEventListener('resize', debouncedUpdate);
   158→
   159→    // Initial measurement after a short delay for layout stability
   160→    const fallbackTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
   161→      if (!hasReceivedStableDimensions.current) {
   162→        updateDimensions();
   163→      }
   164→    }, 100);
   165→
   166→    return () => {
   167→      resizeObserver.disconnect();
   168→      window.removeEventListener('resize', debouncedUpdate);
   169→      clearTimeout(fallbackTimeout);
   170→      if (debounceTimer) clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
   171→    };
   172→  }, [initialWidthEstimate, heightOffset, skipHeaderDetection]);
   173→
   174→  return [containerRef, dimensions];
   175→}
   176→

## MediaGallery Cleanup Plan

Here is a practical, high-impact cleanup plan organized into four independent batches. Each batch can be landed as a single commit with all tests passing.

---

### Batch 1: Delete the re-export layer (net deletion, zero risk)

**What:** Delete `src/features/gallery/components/MediaGallery/` entirely (4 files of pure re-exports + 3 empty directories) and update the 2 consumers to import from `@/shared/components/MediaGallery` directly.

**Files to delete:**
- `src/features/gallery/components/MediaGallery/MediaGallery.tsx` (1 line)
- `src/features/gallery/components/MediaGallery/index.ts` (3 lines)
- `src/features/gallery/components/MediaGallery/hooks/useContainerWidth.ts` (1 line)
- `src/features/gallery/components/MediaGallery/utils/index.ts` (1 line)
- Empty dirs: `components/`, `services/`

**Consumers to update (2 files, 4 import lines):**
- `src/features/gallery/components/GenerationsPane/components/GenerationsPaneGallery.tsx` -- change `from '@/features/gallery/components/MediaGallery'` to `from '@/shared/components/MediaGallery'`
- `src/features/gallery/components/GenerationsPane/hooks/useGenerationsPaneController.ts` -- change the 3 imports from `@/features/gallery/components/MediaGallery` and subpaths to `@/shared/components/MediaGallery` equivalents

**Impact:** Removes an entire indirection layer. Net -6 files, -4 directories. The `GalleryFilterState` and `DEFAULT_GALLERY_FILTERS` re-exports in the features index already point to `@/shared/contracts/galleryFilters`, which is the same place the shared index re-exports from, so imports work identically.

---

### Batch 2: Rename "Optimized" hooks (vestigial suffix removal)

**What:** Rename the two hooks that carry a meaningless "Optimized" suffix. No non-optimized versions exist.

- `useMediaGalleryStateOptimized.ts` -> `useMediaGalleryState.ts`
- `useMediaGalleryFiltersOptimized.ts` -> `useMediaGalleryFilters.ts`

**Internal consumers (only within MediaGallery/):**
- `MediaGallery.tsx` lines 14-15: already aliases `as useMediaGalleryState` / `as useMediaGalleryFilters`, so the aliases become direct imports and the `as` clause is removed.
- No external consumers import these hooks directly (they are internal to the MediaGallery module).

**Impact:** Removes misleading names. The `as` aliases in `MediaGallery.tsx` were a workaround for the bad names; removing them makes the code more direct.

---

### Batch 3: Strip redundant prefixes from util files

**What:** Rename the 4 util files that carry a `mediaGallery-` prefix redundant with their directory path `MediaGallery/utils/`.

| Current | Renamed |
|---|---|
| `mediaGallery-constants.ts` | `constants.ts` |
| `mediaGallery-layout.ts` | `layout.ts` |
| `mediaGallery-scroll.ts` | `scroll.ts` |
| `mediaGallery-scroll.test.ts` | `scroll.test.ts` |
| `mediaGallery-utils.ts` | `utils.ts` |

**Update the barrel `utils/index.ts`** to reference the new filenames. No external consumers import these files by their full path -- they all go through the barrel `utils/index.ts` or import from the component root. One exception: `MediaGallery.tsx` line 35 imports `GRID_COLUMN_CLASSES, calculateGalleryLayout` from `'../utils'` which hits the barrel, so no change needed there.

**Files that need import path updates after rename:**

---

### Batch 4: Extract `useAspectRatioLayout` from MediaGallery.tsx

**What:** The inline `useAspectRatioLayout` function (lines 37-79 in `MediaGallery.tsx`) is a pure layout calculation hook with its own interface. Extract it to `hooks/useAspectRatioLayout.ts`.

This brings `MediaGallery.tsx` from 487 lines to ~445 lines. It is a modest reduction, but importantly it removes the only non-component code from the orchestrator file, making it purely a composition of hooks and JSX.

**Why not do more decomposition of MediaGallery.tsx?** The remaining ~445 lines is the orchestrator body: it composes 10+ hooks and passes their results to 5 sub-components. The JSX render is only ~115 lines. The rest is hook composition and glue logic (creating callbacks like `withPaginationReset`, `handlePageBoundsExceeded`, the `simplifiedShotOptions` memo, the pending lightbox target effect). This glue is inherently tied to this component -- extracting it into yet another hook would just move the same code somewhere else without reducing total complexity. 445 lines for the central orchestrator of a 7000-line module is reasonable.

**Impact:** Clean separation of concerns. The orchestrator becomes purely about composition.

---

### Changes I considered and rejected

1. **Splitting `types.ts` (207 lines):** The file has clear sections (data types, config types, prop types) and is well-organized with interface composition (`MediaGalleryProps = Data & Actions & Pagination & Filters & Display`). Splitting it into 3 files would create import churn across 15+ files that use `GeneratedImageWithMetadata` for minimal clarity gain. The `GalleryFilterState` and `DEFAULT_GALLERY_FILTERS` are already re-exported from `@/shared/contracts/galleryFilters` -- the types.ts just bridges them. 207 lines is fine for a types file.

2. **Renaming hooks to drop `useMediaGallery` prefix:** While the prefix is verbose given the directory, these hooks are imported by name in `MediaGallery.tsx` and it reads well: `useMediaGalleryActions`, `useMediaGalleryPagination`. Renaming to `useActions` or `usePagination` would be ambiguous in import statements and grep results. The verbosity has value here -- it is a naming convention, not redundancy.

3. **Decomposing `useMediaGalleryStateOptimized` (435 lines):** It is a single `useReducer` with a reducer function, initial state factory, action creators, and effects. The reducer (lines 121-233) is 113 lines of straightforward switch cases. The hook body (lines 301-435) is 135 lines of memoized action creators + 3 effects. This is well-structured as-is. The action creators are a flat list generated by `createFieldSetter` -- splitting them into a separate file would scatter related code.

4. **Decomposing `MediaGalleryGrid` (346 lines) or `MediaGalleryItem.tsx` (503 lines):** These are over the 300-line threshold but are separate components with their own decomposition. Not in scope for this cleanup which focuses on the MediaGallery module's structural issues.

---

### Execution order

Batches 1-4 are independent and can be done in any order. I recommend:

1. **Batch 1 first** (re-export deletion) -- most impactful, removes an entire layer
2. **Batch 2** (Optimized rename) -- simple rename, removes confusion
3. **Batch 3** (util prefix cleanup) -- most files touched, but all internal
4. **Batch 4** (extract useAspectRatioLayout) -- smallest win, lowest priority

Total estimated impact: -6 files deleted, 6 files renamed, 1 new file created, ~10 files with import updates. Net reduction of ~5 files and a noticeable reduction in naming noise.

---

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaGallery/hooks/useMediaGalleryStateOptimized.ts` - Rename to drop "Optimized" suffix (Batch 2)
